TiptoBook service catalogue: content for review

Draft content for every service in TiptoBook – Complete Services Catalogue (the PDF), researched on 2026-10-23 from platforms that are live in India today (Urban Company's Patna pages, SevaMart, Sulekha, Justdial, WedMeGood, UrbanPro, NoBroker, brand sites and local Patna operators). All copy is written fresh; the sources are listed under each service so every price can be checked.

Status: for owner review. Nothing here is in the database yet.

How to review

  1. Read a service, then change anything directly in this file: names, wording, packages, prices, FAQs.
  2. Each package line shows PROPOSED price [Patna estimate; metro reference; confidence]. Keep the proposed price, type your own, or delete the package.
  3. Answer the open decisions below (they apply to many services at once).
  4. When you're done, tell me. I turn this file into the database format and write an update script that runs as a dry run first, matches services by slug, never deletes anything, and keeps existing service ids (so current bookings stay linked).

How the prices were set

  • Market: Patna and nearby Bihar. Where a platform publishes Patna prices (mostly Urban Company), those are the anchor; Urban Company's Patna prices are often the same as its metro prices, so local shops are usually 15–30% cheaper.
  • Proposed price: usually at or a little below the Patna estimate. Several categories (men's salon, AC, washing machine) were deliberately priced 20–35% under Urban Company Patna: check your margins before accepting.
  • Confidence: high = a published Patna price; medium = Patna data that is thin or a tier-2 city; low = an estimate from metro data or from parts + labour. Every low price should be confirmed with 2–3 local providers before launch.
  • Never invented: ratings, review counts, customer counts and warranties. Where a warranty is common in the market it is quoted with its source, not promised.

Open decisions (apply across services)

# Decision What the research found Suggestion
1 Service warranty on repairs Urban Company: 30 days (plumbing, AC), 90–180 days on appliance repairs (its own pages contradict each other); SevaMart 7–15 days 30 days on labour; 90 days on gas charging, compressor and replaced parts
2 Visit / inspection charge ₹49–299 depending on the trade; usually adjusted in the bill if work is done ₹99 for home repairs, ₹149–249 for appliances, adjusted in the bill; free site visit for painting inside Patna
3 Parts and material Most platforms charge labour only; parts at shop bill or customer-supplied Labour-only prices; parts at the shop bill, shown to the customer before fitting
4 Travel charge for edge areas (Danapur, Khagaul, Phulwari) Some operators add a flat fee Flat ₹100, or none at launch
5 Minimum order for small items (threading ₹39, face wax ₹79, cupboard lock ₹49) Platforms set a minimum cart value for home visits ₹299 minimum per home visit
6 Maid billing model Locally a maid is paid a monthly salary directly Choose: TiptoBook collects the monthly amount, or a one-time placement fee, or only one-time visits at launch
7 Laundry loss/damage compensation DoorWash Patna: up to 10× the wash charge Up to 10× the service charge
8 Venue booking Platforms don't charge customers; they earn venue commission Free assisted booking; instant online booking "Coming soon"
9 Rental rules not backed by a source Extra hour, 250 km/day outstation minimum, ₹300 driver allowance, waiting ₹100/30 min Confirm with your car partners
10 How services are structured The PDF lists sub-items (e.g. Plumbing → Tap & Faucet Repair…) Each PDF item becomes its own service page with its packages (as drafted here)
11 Existing services not in the PDF Database has: smart locks, online tuition, competitive exam coaching, language classes, cooking service, babysitting, AC check-up, interior/exterior/waterproofing painting Hide them (available: false) rather than delete; or add them to the PDF
12 Waterproofing Big need in Patna's monsoon; Urban Company from ₹4,998 Keep as a separate service (currently in the DB)

1. Vehicle Rental

Wedding Car Rental (category: Vehicle Rental)

  • Suggested slug: wedding-car-rental | Mode: rental | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Decorated car with a dressed driver for baraat, vidaai and reception. Sedan, SUV and luxury options.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Book a flower-decorated car for the groom's baraat, the bride's vidaai or the reception drive. Each booking comes with a driver, fuel and a set block of hours and kilometres, so you know the base cost up front. Pick a sedan for a simple, neat look, an SUV like Innova Crysta or Fortuner for more presence, or a luxury car for a grand entry. We fix the decoration theme with you before the day. Extra hours or kilometres are charged at the rates shown. Book early for the Nov–Feb wedding season.
  • Bullets:
    • Car with driver, fuel and basic flower decoration included
    • 8 hours and 80 km in the base price (wedding operators in Patna commonly give 8 hr / 100 km)
    • Pick a decoration theme in advance; fresh-flower upgrades available
    • Same-day vidaai or reception drop can be added to the same booking
    • Guest cars (Ertiga / Innova) can be booked alongside
  • Packages:
    • Sedan Shaadi Car — Dzire/Amaze/Aura-class sedan, ribbon + artificial/mixed flower decoration, driver, fuel, 8 hr/80 km — 480 min — ₹4,499 [Patna est. ₹5,000 for 8 hr/100 km decorated sedan (kolkatacabservice/nkcabtaxi Patna page); metro ref Delhi from ₹4,999 (Triveni Cabs); confidence medium — one Patna operator rate card, mirrored on a sister site]
    • SUV Baraat Car — Innova Crysta / Ertiga-class, fresh-flower front + bonnet decoration, driver, fuel, 8 hr/80 km — 480 min — ₹7,499 (Ertiga option ₹5,999) [Patna est. Ertiga ₹6,500, Crysta ₹8,000 (kolkatacabservice Patna); confidence medium]
    • Premium SUV — Toyota Fortuner, full fresh-flower decoration, driver, fuel, 8 hr/80 km — 480 min — ₹11,499 [Patna est. ₹12,000 (kolkatacabservice Patna); confidence medium]
    • Luxury Car — Mercedes E-Class / BMW 5 Series / Audi A6 (subject to vendor availability in Patna), premium decoration, 8 hr/80 km — 480 min — from ₹19,999 [Patna est. ₹20,000+ (kolkatacabservice Patna); metro ref Delhi BMW ~₹15k / Audi ~₹18k (Triveni Cabs), Rutz India from ₹22,500 for a 3-hr slot; Commutec luxury from ₹12,000 for 8 hr/80 km (undecorated); confidence low — luxury supply in Patna is thin, confirm with local vendors]
  • Extra charges (proposed for Patna):
    • Extra km: Sedan ₹13/km, Ertiga ₹16/km, Crysta ₹19/km, Fortuner ₹25/km, Luxury ₹60–80/km [Sedan/Ertiga/Crysta rates mirror Patna outstation per-km from Bharat Taxi; Fortuner/luxury are INFERENCE, low confidence]
    • Extra hour: Sedan ₹200, SUV ₹300, Fortuner ₹500, Luxury ₹1,500 [INFERENCE from usual ~10% of base per hour; low confidence]
    • Night charge (car used between 10 PM–6 AM): ₹300 driver night allowance per night [norm: night allowance for 10 PM–6 AM use, Savaari]
    • Outstation wedding (car leaves Patna district): charged per day at the Daily Car Rental outstation per-km rate + driver allowance ₹300/day, plus decoration fee
  • Excludes / charged extra: tolls, state entry tax and parking (paid by customer at actuals); premium decoration beyond the package (e.g. full fresh rose/orchid canopy) ₹1,500–4,000 extra [INFERENCE]; music/DJ in car; driver's food if the event runs past mealtime is customary in Bihar but not compulsory.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Is decoration included? A: Yes, basic decoration is in every package. Fresh-flower or custom themes cost extra, and we confirm the design with you before the wedding day.
    • Q: What if the baraat runs past 8 hours? A: Extra time is billed per hour at the package rate. Driving after 10 PM adds a night allowance for the driver.
    • Q: How early should I book? A: In the Nov–Feb wedding season, book 2–4 weeks ahead. SUVs and luxury cars run out first.
    • Q: Do I need to pay an advance? A: Yes, a part payment confirms the car. Pay the balance online via Razorpay or in cash after the event.
    • Q: Who pays tolls and parking? A: You do, at actual cost. The driver will show receipts.
    • Q: Can you send the car to a wedding outside Patna? A: Yes. Outstation weddings are billed per km plus a daily driver allowance, and the decoration is done before departure.
  • Price unit: event (8 hr / 80 km) + extra km/hour
  • Tags/keywords: wedding car Patna, baraat gaadi, dulha car, shaadi ki gaadi, doli car, vidaai car, sajawat wali gaadi, Fortuner shaadi booking
  • Image brief: Hero — white sedan or SUV with a red-and-white flower arrangement on the bonnet, parked outside a lit-up wedding venue at dusk, driver in a white shirt beside it (faces not shown). Package — close-up of fresh marigold and rose garland decoration on a car bonnet.
  • Sources:
    • https://www.kolkatacabservice.com/bihar/patna/wedding-car — Patna wedding car rates: sedan ₹5,000, SUV/Ertiga from ₹6,500, Crysta ₹8,000, Fortuner ₹12,000, Mercedes/BMW ₹20,000+; includes driver, fuel, 8 hr/100 km, decoration; 50% advance; book 2–4 weeks ahead in Nov–Feb
    • https://www.nkcabtaxi.com/bihar/patna/wedding-car — same Patna ₹5,000 starting price (looks like a sister site, so not independent)
    • https://www.trivenicabs.in/wedding/delhi and https://www.trivenicabs.in/wedding — Delhi metro ref: from ₹4,999; BMW ~₹15k, Audi ~₹18k (headline figures)
    • https://rutzindia.com/wedding-car-rental/ — luxury metro ref: ₹22,500 for a 3-hour slot (Mercedes E / BMW 5 / Audi A6)
    • https://www.commutec.in/services/luxury-car-rental — luxury chauffeur cars from ₹12,000 per 8 hr/80 km (not decorated)
    • https://www.bharattaxi.com/patna — Patna per-km rates used for extra km
    • https://www.savaari.com/delhi/delhi-to-raison-cabs — night allowance applies when the car is used 10 PM–6 AM
    • https://www.happiffie.com/patna/guest-pickup-vehicle-hire — Patna guest pickup costs: about ₹1,500 for one sedan pickup up to ₹18,000+ for an AC bus (supports the add-on guest-car idea)

Daily Car Rental with Driver (category: Vehicle Rental)

  • Suggested slug: car-rental-with-driver | Mode: rental | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): AC car with driver for city errands, airport runs or outstation trips. Pay per package or per km.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Hire an AC car with a driver for a few hours in Patna, a full day of errands, an airport pickup, or a trip to Gaya, Rajgir, Muzaffarpur or beyond. City packages come with a fixed block of hours and kilometres. Outstation trips are billed per km with a daily minimum, plus a fixed allowance for the driver. Tolls, state tax and parking are paid at actual cost, and the driver keeps receipts. Choose a sedan for up to 4 people or an Ertiga, Innova or Crysta for families and luggage.
  • Bullets:
    • AC sedan or SUV with a local driver who knows Patna routes
    • Clear package limits: hours and km shown before you book
    • Fuel included in package and per-km rates
    • Airport and railway station pickup/drop at a fixed fare
    • Pay online (Razorpay) or in cash at the end of the trip
  • Packages (Patna):
    • City Half Day — 4 hr / 40 km, AC sedan (Ertiga/Innova +₹600) — 240 min — ₹1,199 [Patna est. ₹1,100–2,000 (₹2,000 listed by kolkatacabservice; ₹1,100–1,200 is INFERENCE from half of Bharat Taxi's 8 hr rate); metro ref Kolkata sedan ₹12/km (kolkatacabservice); confidence medium]
    • City Full Day — 8 hr / 80 km — 480 min — Sedan ₹1,849 | Ertiga ₹2,599 | Innova ₹2,899 | Innova Crysta ₹3,099 [Patna est. Dzire ₹1,905, Ertiga ₹2,700, Scorpio ₹2,900, Innova ₹3,000, Crysta ₹3,200 (Bharat Taxi Patna); Crysta ₹3,200 + ₹18/extra km (Satyam Travels Patna, search snippet; site now suspended); confidence high for sedan/Innova, medium for others]
    • Airport / Station Transfer — one-way drop or pickup within Patna city (up to 15 km), meet at arrival gate — 60 min — Sedan ₹649 | SUV ₹999 [Patna est. ₹380–1,500 (oneway.cab ₹380, HurryUp Cabs from ₹680, kolkatacabservice ₹1,500+); confidence medium — wide spread between operators]
    • Outstation (round trip) — billed per km, minimum 250 km per calendar day, driver allowance extra — per day — Sedan ₹12/km | Ertiga ₹15/km | Innova ₹16/km | Innova Crysta ₹18/km | Tempo Traveller 12-seat ₹26/km [Patna est. Dzire ₹13, Ertiga ₹16, Scorpio ₹16, Innova ₹17, Crysta ₹19, TT-12 ₹27 (Bharat Taxi); sedan ₹14 (kolkatacabservice); HurryUp Cabs Patna-route extra-km ₹11–29 depending on car; metro ref Kolkata sedan ₹12, SUV ₹16 (kolkatacabservice), Jaipur Innova from ₹12.5 (Savaari); confidence high]
    • Outstation one-way drop — per km, one way only — Sedan ₹12/km | Ertiga ₹14/km | Innova ₹17/km [Patna est. Dzire ₹12, Ertiga ₹14, Innova ₹17, Crysta ₹18 (Bharat Taxi one-way); confidence medium-high]
  • Extra charges / rules (proposed for Patna):
    • Extra km on city packages: Sedan ₹13, Ertiga ₹16, Innova ₹17, Crysta ₹18 [based on Bharat Taxi per-km and Satyam ₹18 Crysta]
    • Extra hour on city packages: Sedan ₹150, SUV ₹200 [INFERENCE; low confidence — confirm with local partners]
    • Driver allowance (outstation): ₹300 per day [common practice; GoSavaari lists ₹400 per day; INFERENCE that ₹250–350 is normal in Bihar]
    • Night charge: ₹300 per night if the car runs between 10 PM and 6 AM [Savaari applies night driver allowance for 10 PM–6 AM]
    • Minimum 250 km per day on outstation round trips [INFERENCE: common industry norm; not confirmed from a cited page]
    • Tolls, state entry tax (e.g. into UP/Jharkhand) and parking: customer pays at actuals [GoSavaari lists toll, state tax and parking as extra; Savaari leaves these out of round-trip and local fares]
  • Excludes / charged extra: tolls, interstate permit/state tax, parking (airport/station parking too), driver night stay is on the driver (allowance covers it), extra hours/km, waiting beyond 30 min on transfers (₹100 per 30 min, INFERENCE).
  • FAQs:
    • Q: What does 8 hr / 80 km mean? A: You get the car and driver for up to 8 hours or 80 km. If you go over either limit, extra hours or km are billed at the listed rates.
    • Q: Who pays tolls and parking? A: You do, at actual cost. The driver pays on the way and adds them to the final bill with receipts.
    • Q: How is an outstation trip billed? A: Per km for the full distance (minimum 250 km a day), plus ₹300 a day for the driver. Tolls and state tax are extra.
    • Q: Is there a night charge? A: Yes, ₹300 per night if the car is driving between 10 PM and 6 AM.
    • Q: Can I change the pickup time? A: Yes, if you tell us at least 3 hours before. The car is held for 30 minutes after the booked time on airport transfers.
    • Q: Can I pay in cash? A: Yes. Pay online via Razorpay when booking or in cash to the driver at the end of the trip.
  • Price unit: package (hour+km) for local; km for outstation; job for airport transfer
  • Tags/keywords: car rental Patna with driver, gaadi booking Patna, taxi Patna, Innova booking Patna, airport cab Patna, bahar jaane ke liye gaadi, din bhar ke liye gaadi, Patna to Gaya taxi
  • Image brief: Hero — clean white sedan parked on a Patna road (e.g. near Gandhi Maidan or a generic tree-lined street) with a uniformed driver holding the rear door (face not visible). Package — Innova-style MPV with luggage being loaded at an airport curb.
  • Sources:
    • https://www.bharattaxi.com/patna — Patna 8 hr/80 km rates (Dzire ₹1,905, Innova ₹3,000, Crysta ₹3,200, Scorpio ₹2,900, Ertiga ₹2,700); outstation and one-way per-km rates
    • https://www.satyamtravel.com/pricing — (search snippet only; site now suspended) Crysta local ₹3,200 for 8 hr/80 km, extra km ₹18
    • https://www.kolkatacabservice.com/bihar/patna/wedding-car — Patna local ₹2,000 for 4 hr/40 km, outstation ₹14/km, airport ₹1,500+
    • https://www.kolkatacabservice.com/ — Kolkata sedan ₹12/km, SUV ₹16/km (metro ref)
    • https://hurryupcabs.com/cabs/airport/patna-airport — Patna airport taxi from ₹680; Patna-route extra-km rates ₹11–29 by car type
    • https://oneway.cab/patna/patna-to-patna-airport-taxi/fare — Patna city to airport from ₹380 (snippet)
    • https://www.savaari.com/jaipur/innova-cabs-jaipur — Innova from ₹12.5/km (tier-2 metro ref)
    • https://www.savaari.com/delhi/delhi-to-raison-cabs — night driver allowance applies for 10 PM–6 AM use
    • https://www.gosavaari.in/tariff — driver allowance ₹400; toll, state tax, parking extra
    • https://www.solocabs.com/patna — Patna local packages offered as 8 hr/80 km and 12 hr/120 km; headline "from ₹9/km"

Summary:

  • Items done: Wedding Car Rental (4 tiers + extra km/hr/night rules); Daily Car Rental with Driver (half day, 8 hr/80 km, airport transfer, outstation round trip + one-way per km, driver allowance, night charge, toll/parking rules).
  • Not covered: self-drive rental (Zoomcar/Revv) and tempo traveller/bus guest shuttles beyond the TT per-km rate; no Urban Company equivalent exists for this category.
  • Biggest uncertainty: luxury wedding car supply/price in Patna (₹20k+ comes from one operator) and the extra-hour rates, the 250 km/day minimum and the ₹300 driver allowance, which are inferred and not confirmed on a Patna page.

2. Wedding & Event

Photography, decoration, venues

Wedding & Event Photography / Videography (category: Wedding & Events)

  • Suggested slug: wedding-photography-videography | Mode: visit (on-site at venue/home) | Status: live (enquiry + advance booking; final quote after call)
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Photographers and videographers for weddings, pre-wedding shoots, tilak, birthdays and family functions in Patna.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Book a photo and video team for your wedding day, pre-wedding shoot or family function. Choose plain traditional coverage, or add candid photos and a short cinematic film. Teams come with their own cameras, lights and edit the photos and video after the event. You get edited photos online first, and a printed album if you add one. Drone shots can be added where the venue and local rules allow. Tell us the date, venue, number of functions and hours, and we confirm the team and final price before you pay the advance.
  • Bullets:
    • Traditional, candid or mixed photo coverage
    • Full event video plus a short highlight film
    • Edited photos shared online; printed album as add-on
    • Pre-wedding shoot at a Patna location of your choice
    • Optional drone shots, subject to venue/local permission
  • Packages:
    • Family Function Shoot — 1 traditional photographer, up to 4 hrs, 150+ edited photos online — 240 min — ₹4,999 [Patna est. under ₹10,000/day for budget photographers (wedding.net lists 19 such in Patna); metro ref ₹30,000+/day (WedMeGood Delhi); confidence medium — budget tier exists but per-hour pricing not listed]
    • Wedding Day Traditional Photo + Video — 1 photographer + 1 videographer, one function up to ~10 hrs, edited photos + full video + 3–5 min highlight — 600 min — ₹24,999 [Patna est. ₹10,000–35,000 for photo only (wedding.net budget filter; Blushing Bride photo ₹35,000/day on WedMeGood); metro ref ₹33,000 photo+video/day (The Vow Tales, Delhi, WeddingBazaar) to ₹1,00,000 (Suvi Studio, Delhi); confidence medium-low]
    • Candid + Cinematic Wedding — candid photographer + traditional photographer + cinematographer, one day, cinematic film + highlight reel — 720 min — ₹54,999 [Patna est. ~₹70,000/day photo+video for a well-reviewed studio (Blushing Bride, WedMeGood); metro ref ₹1,00,000–1,50,000/day (Suvi Studio; Weddings By Lalit, Delhi, WedMeGood); confidence medium]
    • Pre-Wedding Shoot — 1 photographer (+ video optional), half day, 1–2 Patna locations, 50+ edited photos — 300 min — ₹14,999 [Patna est. ₹25,000/day (Blushing Bride, WedMeGood); metro ref ₹14,999 (The Wedding Wire, Kolkata) to ₹75,000 (Weddings By Lalit, Delhi); confidence medium]
    • Add-on: Printed album 40 pages — ₹8,999 [Patna est. ₹10,000 per 40 pages (Blushing Bride, WedMeGood); confidence medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: drone operator and any permits; extra hours/functions (haldi, tilak, reception counted separately); printed album; travel and stay outside Patna city (e.g. Gaya, Muzaffarpur); location entry fees for pre-wedding shoots; raw footage drive.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Is each function priced separately? A: Yes. Tilak, haldi, wedding and reception are usually separate days or slots. Tell us all functions and we quote them together.
    • Q: When do we get photos and video? A: Edited photos usually come first and the film later. We confirm exact delivery dates with the team at booking. [INFERENCE: no single platform timeline found]
    • Q: Can you shoot with a drone? A: Yes, as an add-on, if the venue allows it and the area is not restricted. Areas near Patna airport may need special permission.
    • Q: What is the difference between candid and traditional? A: Traditional means posed group photos and full ritual coverage. Candid means natural, unposed moments. Most families book both.
    • Q: How much advance do we pay? A: A part payment blocks the date; the rest is due on or before the event. Exact split is set at booking.
    • Q: Do you cover weddings outside Patna? A: Yes, across nearby Bihar. Travel and stay are charged extra.
  • Price unit: event (per day / per function); album per piece

4. Home Services & Repair

CCTV Installation

Research note: Urban Company does not list a CCTV service in search results (only TV repair, visit fee ₹249 adjusted in final quote). The only structured Patna price list found is Smart City Care (a multi-city lead-gen installer; numbers marked "indicative"). Cross-checked against SBM Technologies (Chennai, itemised labour) and Eye Tech Securities (Chennai kit packages). Treat Patna figures as medium/low confidence until checked with 2–3 local Patna installers (e.g. Boring Road / Kankarbagh CP Plus dealers). Pricing model recommended: book LABOUR on TiptoBook as fixed packages; quote HARDWARE separately as an optional kit (brand + model + MRP shown) so the customer can also supply their own cameras.

Home CCTV Setup (category: Home Services & Repair)

  • Suggested slug: home-cctv-installation | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Wired CCTV fitting for your house with DVR, hard disk and live view on your phone.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): A technician visits, checks where cameras are needed, and fits 2, 4 or 8 wired cameras with a recorder (DVR, or NVR for IP cameras). Cables are run along walls or through existing conduit, the hard disk is formatted, recording is switched on, and the brand app is set up on your phone so you can watch live and play back footage. You can buy a camera kit through us (CP Plus, Hikvision or Dahua) or give us cameras you already bought. Labour is priced per package; hardware and extra cable are billed separately.
  • Bullets (3–5, what the customer gets):
    • Site check and camera position plan before drilling
    • Camera mounting, cable routing, DVR/NVR and hard disk setup
    • Recording, motion alerts and mobile app live view set up on up to 2 phones

    • Works with CP Plus, Hikvision, Dahua and similar brands

    • 2-camera install — mount 2 cameras, up to 30 m cable laying, DVR + HDD setup, app on phone — 120 min — ₹999 [Patna est. labour ₹850–1,300; metro ref ₹300/camera + ₹1,000 DVR config = ₹1,600 (Chennai, SBM); confidence medium]

    • 8-camera install — mount 8 cameras, up to 120 m cable laying, 8-ch DVR/NVR setup, app on 2 phones — 1 day (360–480 min) — ₹3,499 [Patna est. labour ₹3,700–7,900; metro ref 8×₹300 + ₹1,500 = ₹3,900 (Chennai, SBM); confidence medium]
    • Optional HARDWARE KITS (quote separately, show brand/model): 2-cam HD kit ≈ ₹7,500–10,000; 4-cam HD kit (4 cams + 4-ch DVR + 1 TB HDD + cable + power supply) ≈ ₹11,000–15,000; 8-cam kit ≈ ₹20,000–28,000 [Patna component sums from Smart City Care: 2-cam ₹8,700–14,850 incl. labour, 4-cam ₹11,650–23,400 incl. labour; all-India 2-cam ₹8k–14k, 4-cam ₹14k–22k, 8-cam ₹28k–42k incl. labour; Chennai 4-cam all-in ₹14,500–18,000; confidence medium-low — CP Plus/Hikvision dealer prices move often, re-check monthly]

    • Q: Which brand should I pick? A: CP Plus, Hikvision and Dahua are the common brands sold and serviced in Patna. Spare parts and local repair are easy to find for all three.
    • Q: How many days of recording will I get? A: It depends on hard disk size, number of cameras and resolution. A 1 TB disk with 4 HD cameras usually holds roughly 2–3 weeks; the technician will show the exact figure. [INFERENCE — verify with brand storage calculator]
    • Q: Will I see the cameras on my phone? A: Yes, if the DVR is connected to your Wi-Fi router or internet line. We install the brand app and add the device for you.
    • Q: Does it work during power cuts? A: Not by itself. Add a small UPS or inverter connection to keep the DVR and cameras running.
    • Q: Do you do hidden wiring? A: Yes, after a site check. Concealed wiring needs wall chasing and is quoted per metre.
  • Price unit: job (per package); extra cable per metre

CCTV Repair & Maintenance (category: Home Services & Repair)

  • Suggested slug: cctv-repair-maintenance | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Technician visit to fix no-video, blurry picture, DVR, cable or mobile-view problems, plus yearly AMC.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): When a camera shows a blank screen, the DVR stops recording, or the app goes offline, a technician comes to find the fault. The visit fee covers diagnosis; you approve the repair quote before any work starts, and the visit fee is adjusted in the final bill if you go ahead. Common fixes include loose connectors, failed power supplies, cable faults, hard disk errors and app reconnection. For homes and shops with many cameras, a yearly maintenance plan (AMC) covers scheduled cleaning, health checks and priority visits. Spare parts are charged at printed price.
  • Bullets:
    • Fault check of cameras, DVR/NVR, hard disk, power supply and cables
    • Repair quote shared before work; visit fee adjusted if you go ahead
    • Lens cleaning, connector re-crimping and camera angle reset
    • Mobile app reconnection and password reset (owner proof needed)
    • AMC option: scheduled service visits and priority call-outs
  • Packages:
    • Visit & diagnosis — technician visit, full system check, written repair quote — 45–60 min — ₹299 (adjusted in repair bill) [Patna est. inspection ₹300–650; metro ref: UC uses a similar "visit fee adjusted in final quote" model for TV repair at ₹249 (Delhi NCR); confidence medium]
    • Standard fix (labour) — one fault: connector/BNC re-crimp, power supply swap, re-angle, HDD format, app reconnect, up to 2 cameras cleaned — 60–90 min — ₹549 + parts [Patna est. cabling repair ₹550–1,000, network config ₹450–1,150, repositioning ₹400–700; confidence medium]
    • DVR/NVR & software service — firmware update, HDD replace/format, password reset, remote view fix — 60–120 min — ₹799 + parts [Patna est. DVR/NVR repair ₹1,500–3,200 (incl. parts), software update ₹600–1,100; confidence medium-low]
    • Yearly AMC (up to 4 cameras + 1 DVR) — 4 quarterly visits: cleaning, health check, recording test, app check; priority call-outs; labour for routine faults included, parts extra — 12 months — ₹1,999/year; add ₹299 per extra camera [Patna est. ₹2,500–5,200/year; metro ref ₹450 per HD camera/DVR per year (NiceDeal), ₹1,500 flat (Secure Home Solutions), ~₹4,000/yr (IndiaMART listing); confidence medium-low]

[INFERENCE on AMC exclusions — set your own policy]
  • FAQs:
    • Q: What is the ₹299 visit fee for? A: It covers travel and a full fault check. If you approve the repair, it is adjusted in the final bill.
    • Q: My cameras show "No Video". What usually causes it? A: Most often a loose connector, a dead power adapter or a damaged cable. Less often the camera itself has failed.
    • Q: I forgot my DVR password. Can you reset it? A: Yes. We need proof that you own the system (purchase bill or ID at the site address). Some brands need a reset code from the brand.
    • Q: Do you repair systems installed by someone else? A: Yes. We service CP Plus, Hikvision, Dahua and most other brands, whoever installed them.
    • Q: What does the AMC cover? A: Four planned visits a year, cleaning, recording and app checks, and labour for routine faults. Parts are charged extra.
  • Price unit: visit (diagnosis); job (repair); year (AMC)
  • Tags/keywords: cctv repair patna, camera band ho gaya, dvr no video, cctv theek karna, cctv mobile pe nahi dikh raha, hard disk error dvr, cctv amc, camera saaf karna
  • Image brief: Hero — technician with a small tester/monitor checking a DVR on a shelf, cables neatly labelled. Package photo — close-up of hands crimping a BNC connector, or technician cleaning a dome camera lens on a ladder.

Plumbing

Category-wide norms (apply to all items below)

  • Visit / inspection: Urban Company (UC) charges ₹49 for a plumber consultation in Patna (quote given on arrival); UC waives its visitation fee on orders above ₹499. Proposed TiptoBook: ₹99 inspection visit, adjusted against the bill if work is done. Sources: https://www.urbancompany.com/patna-plumbers (₹49 consultation, "visitation fee off on orders above ₹499").
  • Material: UC lets the customer buy parts themselves or have the plumber fetch them; time spent fetching counts as work time. Proposed TiptoBook rule: all prices are labour only; parts at MRP with bill, or customer-supplied. Source: UC Patna FAQ (same URL).
  • Warranty norm: UC states a 30-day guarantee on all plumbing services plus damage cover up to ₹10,000 (UC policy, not ours). Do NOT copy the warranty unless TiptoBook decides its own. Source: UC Patna page.
  • Local Patna price point: Service On Wheel Patna lists tap repair ₹300 (MRP ₹350), tap replacement ₹300, jet spray install/repair ₹350, external PVC flush tank repair ₹399, waste pipe leakage ₹400. Source: https://www.serviceonwheel.com/patna/service/plumber.html (seen in search snippet; page returned 404 on direct fetch — medium reliability).
  • UC Patna "starts at" prices are much lower than UC Delhi (e.g. tap repair ₹49 vs ₹129) — likely promotional entry prices. Real Patna local rates sit between UC Patna and Service On Wheel.

Tap & Faucet Repair (category: Plumbing)

  • Suggested slug: tap-faucet-repair | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Fix leaking, dripping or loose taps, or fit a new tap or sink mixer you have bought.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): A dripping tap wastes water and raises the motor's running time. Our plumber checks the tap, finds the cause (worn washer, spindle, cartridge or loose body) and repairs it on the spot. If the tap is beyond repair, we fit a new one, either brought by you or bought by us at the shop bill price. We also fit kitchen sink mixers, wall-mixers, extension nozzles and aerators. Price is per tap, labour only. Parts like washers or cartridges are charged at actual cost.
  • Bullets:
    • Diagnosis and repair of dripping or leaking taps
    • Washer, spindle or cartridge change (part extra)
    • New tap or sink mixer fitting with thread tape sealing
    • Leak test after work and area wiped clean
  • Packages:
    • Tap repair (per tap) — washer/spindle/cartridge change, tightening, leak test — 30 min — ₹99 [Patna est. ₹49–300 (UC Patna from ₹49; Service On Wheel Patna ₹300); metro ref UC Delhi from ₹129; confidence medium: two Patna sources but wide gap]
    • Tap installation / replacement (per tap) — remove old tap, fit new one, seal, test — 30 min — ₹129 [Patna est. ₹59–300 (UC Patna from ₹59; SOW Patna ₹300); metro ref UC Delhi from ₹129; confidence medium]
    • Sink / wall mixer installation — fit kitchen or basin mixer, connect hoses — 45 min — ₹249 [Patna est. no direct listing; UC Patna shower mixer ₹349, Delhi ₹249 used as proxy; confidence low]
    • Tap combo (any 3 taps repair/replace) — 60 min — ₹249 [Patna est. derived from per-tap rate; UC Patna lists a tap & mixer combo from ₹49; confidence low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: new tap/mixer, washers, cartridges, connection hoses, angle valves; wall/tile breaking for concealed lines; ₹99 inspection if no work is done.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Do I need to buy the tap first? A: No. You can buy it yourself, or the plumber can buy it locally and you pay the shop bill.
    • Q: My tap still drips after closing tightly. Can it be fixed? A: Usually yes. Most drips come from a worn washer or cartridge, which is a cheap part.
    • Q: Is the price per tap? A: Yes, labour is per tap. The 3-tap combo is cheaper if several taps need work.
    • Q: Can you fix a concealed (in-wall) tap body? A: The plumber will inspect and quote. If tiles must be opened, that is quoted separately before starting.
    • Q: Will water supply be stopped? A: Briefly. The plumber will close the main valve or stop the motor during work.
  • Price unit: job (per tap)

Shower & Mixer Repair (category: Plumbing)

  • Suggested slug: shower-mixer-repair | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Repair or fit showers, hand showers, diverters and bathroom wall mixers for steady hot and cold water.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Low flow, a stuck diverter or a shower head that leaks at the joint are common problems, often caused by hard water scale. Our plumber cleans or repairs the shower head, fixes leaks at the arm and hose, and repairs or replaces the diverter or mixer cartridge. We also fit new overhead showers, hand showers, shower filters and wall-mounted hot-and-cold mixers. Prices cover labour; any new shower, hose or cartridge is billed at cost or supplied by you.
  • Bullets:
    • Shower head descaling and leak fixing
    • Diverter / mixer cartridge repair or change (part extra)
    • New overhead shower, hand shower or shower filter fitting
    • Wall mixer installation on existing hot and cold points
    • Flow and leak check after work
  • Packages:
    • Shower repair — clean/descale head, fix arm or hose leak — 30 min — ₹99 [Patna est. ₹99 (UC Patna ₹99); metro ref UC Delhi ₹99; confidence high: same price on both UC pages]
    • Shower installation (overhead or hand shower) — 30 min — ₹119 [Patna est. from ₹99 (UC Patna); metro ref UC Delhi from ₹129; confidence medium]

    • Shower mixer / diverter installation or cartridge change — 60 min — ₹299 [Patna est. ₹349 (UC Patna shower mixer tap install); metro ref UC Delhi ₹249; confidence medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: shower set, hose, cartridge, diverter spindle, filter; geyser repair (separate service); new concealed hot/cold lines or tile cutting.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Why is my shower flow weak? A: Usually scale blocks the nozzles or the filter. Cleaning often fixes it; low tank height can also reduce pressure.
    • Q: Can you fit a mixer if I only have a cold line? A: A hot-and-cold mixer needs both lines. Adding a hot line is a new-fitting job and is quoted separately.
    • Q: Do you repair branded mixers like Jaquar or Cera? A: Yes, labour is the same. Brand cartridges must match the model, so bring the old one or the model number.
    • Q: Does a shower filter help with Patna's hard water? A: It reduces chlorine and some sediment. Filter cartridges need regular change; follow the maker's guidance.
    • Q: Is geyser repair included? A: No. Only the water connection to the geyser is covered under plumbing.
  • Price unit: job

    • Fixing of taps, WC, basin and shower after tiling (if booked)
  • Packages:
    • Site survey & quote — point count, material list — 45 min — ₹199, adjusted in final bill [no direct comparable; UC consultation ₹49 is for small jobs; confidence low]
    • Bathroom plumbing, exposed (labour only, up to 10 points) — 1–2 days — ₹4,999 [Patna est. ₹4,500–8,000: budget labour ₹550–800 per supply point for metro + tier-2, and tier-2 labour runs 20–35% below metro; metro ref ₹7,000–15,000 labour per bathroom; confidence low — no Patna listing found]
    • Bathroom plumbing, concealed (labour only, up to 10 points) — 2–3 days — ₹6,999 [Patna est. ₹6,000–10,000 (concealed adds 35–60% over exposed per Studio Matrx); metro ref concealed supply point labour ₹800–1,800; confidence low]
    • Kitchen plumbing (sink, RO, washing machine; up to 5 points) — 1 day — ₹2,499; extra point ₹449 [Patna est. derived from per-point labour above; HouseYog lists labour ₹360–600 per point; kitchen sink plumbing all-in ₹4,000–7,000 incl. material; confidence low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: CPVC/UPVC pipes and fittings (₹1,500–3,500 CPVC + ₹1,200–3,000 UPVC per bathroom per Studio Matrx), valves, sanitaryware and CP fittings, wall chasing and re-plastering/tiling (mason), debris removal, soil stack work, overhead tank and motor.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: What is a "point"? A: Each place water comes out or drains, e.g. a basin with hot and cold is two supply points plus one drain.
    • Q: Concealed or exposed — which is better? A: Concealed looks neater but costs more and needs tile breaking for future repairs. Exposed is cheaper and easy to reach.
    • Q: Is material included? A: No. We give a material list; you buy it or we buy at bill price. Branded CPVC is advised.
    • Q: Do you test before tiling? A: Yes. Lines are pressure-tested before walls are closed, so leaks are caught early.
    • Q: Can you replace old GI pipes? A: Yes. Old GI supply lines can be swapped for CPVC; the survey decides how much wall must be opened.
    • Q: How is payment made? A: Usually an advance after the quote and the balance on completion, online or cash.
  • Price unit: job (per bathroom / per kitchen; extra points per point)
  • Tags/keywords: bathroom fitting Patna, plumbing kaam, naya pipe fitting, CPVC pipe fitting, ghar ka plumbing, concealed fitting, bathroom pipe line, plumber thekedar
  • Image brief: Hero — unfinished bathroom wall with orange-tan CPVC pipes in chased grooves, plumber checking a joint. Package — kitchen sink cabinet showing neat inlet, RO and waste connections.
  • Sources: https://www.studiomatrx.org/guides/bathroom-plumbing-cost-india — points definition, 8–12 points per bathroom, per-point labour bands, concealed premium, tier-2 20–35% below metro, material ranges, pressure test; https://www.scribd.com/document/430059548/Plumbing-Labour-Rates — ₹7,000–15,000 labour per bathroom (older document, date unclear); https://www.houseyog.com/blog/cost-of-plumbing-in-new-construction-per-bathroom-amp-whole-house-india-2025-guide — labour ₹360–600 per point, kitchen sink plumbing ₹4,000–7,000.

Electrician

Shared Patna facts (all items): Urban Company (UC) runs electrician services in Patna with published per-job prices (https://www.urbancompany.com/patna-electricians). UC FAQ on that page: no booking fee, but a minimum ₹99 + 18% GST if the electrician only inspects/advises and no work is done; a "visitation fee" is waived on orders above ₹499; it advertises a 30-day post-service guarantee and damage protection up to ₹10,000 (not on cash payments). UC also lists an "Electrician consultation" at ₹49 (35 min, quote given on arrival) in Patna. Spare parts are extra on UC. Proposed TiptoBook visit-charge norm: ₹99 inspection/visit charge, adjusted against the bill if any job is booked; waived when the order is ≥ ₹499. Quote given before work starts. (Matches UC Patna norm; confidence high.)

Full Home Wiring (category: Electrician)

  • Suggested slug: home-wiring-rewiring | Mode: doorstep (visit + quote for big jobs) | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): New points, extra wiring runs or full house rewiring, open (casing) or concealed in the wall.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Need a new plug point, a fresh line to the kitchen, or a full rewiring of an old house? Our electrician first checks the load, existing wires and the main board, then gives you a written quote before any work begins. Small jobs like adding wire in PVC casing are charged per 5 metres. New points are charged per point. Full home rewiring is quoted per point or per square foot after a site visit. You can buy the material yourself or ask us to bring ISI-marked wire, conduit and switches at bill price.
  • Bullets:
    • Site visit and written quote before work starts
    • Open wiring in PVC casing or concealed wiring in wall chase
    • Per-point pricing for new light, fan and socket points
    • Load check so AC/geyser lines get the right wire size
    • Old wire removed and connections tested after the job
  • Packages:
    • Wiring visit & estimate — check of wiring, DB/MCB and load; written quote for rewiring — 30 min — ₹99 (adjusted in final bill) [Patna est. ₹49–99 (UC consultation ₹49; UC minimum ₹99+GST); metro ref UC Mumbai minimum ₹99+GST; confidence high]
    • Open wiring in PVC casing (per 5 m) — run new wire inside surface casing along wall, connect to existing point; labour only — 30 min — ₹199 [Patna est. UC ₹219 per 5 m with casing, ₹99 per 5 m without casing; metro ref UC Bengaluru "Wiring" ₹129 start; confidence high]
    • Concealed new point (per point, labour only) — wall chasing, conduit laying, wire pulling, box fixing, switch connection for one light/fan/6A socket point; plaster patch not included — 60–90 min — ₹249 per point (16A/AC/geyser power point ₹349) [Patna est. ₹180–350 per point (tier-2 data: Orai light point ₹180–250, fan point ₹250–350, AC point ₹400–600; national labour-only ₹180–350); metro ref Mumbai/Pune primary light point ₹280, full plug point ₹350; confidence medium — no Patna-specific per-point source found]
    • Full home rewiring (labour only) — remove old wiring, new concealed/open wiring for all points, DB connection and testing; quoted after visit — 2–5 days — from ₹220 per point (typical 2BHK 50–75 points ≈ ₹11,000–16,500 labour) [national labour-only ₹180–350/point; with material ₹700–1,600/point or ₹90–200 per sq ft concealed (clyfthome 2026); confidence medium-low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: wire, conduit, casing, switches, boxes, MCB/DB, RCCB (customer buys or we supply at bill price); wall plaster patch and paint after chasing; earthing pit (typ. ₹4,000–12,000 per pit, clyfthome); scaffolding for high ceilings; debris removal.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: What is the difference between open and concealed wiring? A: Open wiring runs on the wall inside a PVC casing. It is quicker and cheaper. Concealed wiring runs inside a groove cut into the wall, so it is neater but needs plaster repair.
    • Q: Do I need to buy the wire myself? A: Your choice. You can buy it, or we bring ISI-marked copper wire and branded switches and add the shop bill to your invoice.
    • Q: How is a full rewiring priced? A: After a site visit we count the points (lights, fans, sockets, AC lines) and quote per point. You see the full amount before work starts.
    • Q: How long does rewiring a 2BHK take? A: Usually 2 to 5 working days, depending on concealed vs open wiring, wall type and how many points need changing.
    • Q: Will you repair the wall after chasing? A: We fill the groove with basic patch. Final plaster finish and painting are extra or can be booked separately.
    • Q: Is there a visit charge? A: ₹99 for inspection and quote. It is adjusted in your bill if you book the work.
  • Price unit: point (rewiring); per 5 m (casing runs); visit (estimate)
  • Tags/keywords: house wiring Patna, rewiring, concealed wiring, casing wiring, naya point lagwana, bijli wiring, ghar ki wiring, wiring mistri
  • Image brief: Hero — electrician in uniform pulling copper wire through a wall conduit in a half-finished room, toolbox and modular switchboard visible. Package photo — neat white PVC casing running along a painted wall to a new socket.
  • Sources: https://www.urbancompany.com/patna-electricians — Patna per-5 m wiring prices (₹99/₹219/₹179), ₹99+GST minimum, ₹49 consultation, visitation fee off above ₹499, 30-day guarantee; https://sitesetu.app/downloads/templates/electrical-rate-list-format.pdf — Orai per-point labour (light ₹180–250, fan ₹250–350, AC ₹400–600, DB ₹800–1,500) and Mumbai/Pune point rates; https://clyfthome.com/blog/electrical-wiring-cost-india — national labour-only ₹180–350/point, with material ₹700–1,600, ₹90–200/sq ft concealed, 2BHK 50–75 points, earth pit ₹4,000–12,000; https://www.urbancompany.com/bangalore-electricians — metro wiring ₹129 start; https://www.urbancompany.com/mumbai-electricians — ₹99+GST minimum.

Inverter & UPS (category: Electrician)

  • Suggested slug: inverter-ups-service | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Inverter and home UPS installation, battery swap, house wiring for backup, check-up and repair.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Power cuts are common, so a working inverter matters. Our electrician installs a new inverter or home UPS with its battery, connects it to the lines you want on backup, and checks charging and changeover. We also move or remove old units, replace batteries, top up distilled water, clean terminals and find faults when backup time drops or the unit beeps. You buy the inverter and battery, or we help you choose. Repairs are quoted after a check-up, before any part is changed.
  • Bullets:
    • New inverter/UPS and battery set up and tested
    • Backup wiring to selected lights, fans and sockets
    • Battery swap with terminal cleaning and polarity check
    • Service: water top-up, terminal cleaning, charging and load test
    • Fault check with a quote before any repair
  • Packages:
    • Inverter check-up & service — battery water top-up (customer's distilled water), terminal cleaning, charging/backup test — 45–60 min — ₹149 [Patna est. UC ₹149; tier-2 UC Lucknow inverter check-up ₹160; metro ref UC Bengaluru inverter check-up/service/install from ₹179; confidence high]
    • Inverter/UPS installation (existing backup wiring) — place unit and battery, connect to mains and backup line, set battery type, test — 60 min — ₹299 (uninstall/shift ₹199) [Patna est. UC install/uninstall from ₹299; metro ref UC Bengaluru from ₹179; confidence high]
    • Battery replacement — remove old battery, fit new one, clean and grease terminals, test charging — 30–45 min — ₹199 [Patna est. ₹150–300 — INFERENCE from UC Patna install ₹299 being a larger job; no direct source for battery-only labour; note many battery shops include fitting when you buy from them; confidence low]
    • Inverter backup wiring (new house line) — separate wiring from inverter to chosen points, changeover set-up — 2–4 hrs — from ₹699 for up to 5 points, + ₹129 per extra point; wire extra [Patna est. built from UC Patna wiring ₹99–219 per 5 m and tier-2 point rates ₹180–350; confidence medium-low]
    • Inverter repair (diagnosis + labour) — find fault, fuse/relay/MCB fix; card-level repair at workshop quoted separately — 60 min — ₹199 (fuse replacement ₹99) [Patna est. UC repair from ₹209, fuse ₹99; confidence high]
  • Excludes / charged extra: inverter, battery, distilled water, fuses, relays, PCB/card repair, wire and changeover switch; battery trolley; transport of unit to workshop; old battery disposal (customer may exchange at dealer).
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Do I need separate wiring for an inverter? A: If your house has no backup line, yes. We can wire selected lights and fans to the inverter, or connect the whole house through a changeover if load allows.
    • Q: How often should the battery be serviced? A: Check the water level regularly, more often in summer, and keep terminals clean. Top up only with distilled water (Exide and Eastman advise this). Our technician can show you how.
    • Q: My backup time has dropped. Is the battery dead? A: Not always. Low water, loose terminals or wrong charging settings can cause it. A check-up tells you whether to service or replace.
    • Q: Do you sell inverters or batteries? A: You buy them from any shop or brand. We install and connect them. We can suggest the right capacity for your load.
    • Q: Will brand warranty stay valid? A: Brand warranty follows the brand's own terms. Keep your purchase bill and check whether the brand asks for its own installer before booking us.
  • Price unit: job (per unit / per visit); backup wiring per point
  • Tags/keywords: inverter installation Patna, inverter battery change, inverter wiring, UPS lagwana, inverter theek karna, battery mein pani dalna, inverter service, Luminous/Exide inverter fitting
  • Image brief: Hero — technician connecting cables to a home inverter on a battery trolley in a utility corner. Package photo — gloved hands topping up distilled water in a tubular battery.
  • Sources: https://www.urbancompany.com/patna-electricians — Patna inverter install/uninstall from ₹299, check-up/servicing ₹149, repair ₹209, fuse ₹99, stabiliser install ₹139; https://www.urbancompany.com/lucknow-inverter-repair — tier-2 inverter check-up ₹160, "upto 30 days warranty" (search snippet); https://www.urbancompany.com/bangalore-electricians — metro inverter service/install from ₹179; https://www.exidecare.com/faq/inverter-battery-faq — check electrolyte level and top up with distilled water (search snippet); https://eaplworld.com/debunked-6-inverter-battery-myths-you-should-stop-believing — distilled water is the right top-up (search snippet); https://www.luminousindia.com/installation — Luminous online orders delivered/installed via aligned partners (search snippet; free/not free not confirmed).

For new built-in wardrobes, the carpenter measures on site and gives a written quote per sq ft. You can choose labour-only (you buy plywood, laminate and hardware) or a full job with material. The quote lists ply grade, laminate, hinge and channel brand so you can compare.
  • Bullets:
    • Hinge (normal or soft-close) and drawer channel replacement
    • Cupboard and drawer lock repair or change
    • Shelf fitting and shutter alignment
    • New wardrobe: labour-only or with material, quoted per sq ft
    • Written quote that splits labour and material
  • Packages:
    • Hinge repair/replace (per shutter, labour) — 20–30 min — ₹129 [Patna est. ₹80–200; metro ref UC from ₹149; confidence medium]
    • Drawer channel repair/replace (per drawer, labour) — 30 min — ₹169 [Patna est. ₹100–250; metro ref UC from ₹199; confidence medium]
    • Cupboard/drawer lock change (per lock, labour) — 15 min — ₹49 [Patna est. ₹50–100; metro ref UC from ₹49; confidence medium]
    • New wardrobe, labour only — per sq ft of front area — ₹150/sq ft [Patna est. ₹120–170; tier-2 ref Indore ₹140–190; metro ₹180–240; confidence medium]
    • New wardrobe, with material (BWR ply + 1 mm laminate + standard hardware) — per sq ft — ₹1,250/sq ft [Patna est. ₹1,100–1,800; national ₹1,300–2,200; HomeLane Patna 6-ft modular ₹65,000–95,000; confidence low-medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: hinges, channels, locks, handles, plywood, laminate, edge-banding, mirrors, lofts (quoted separately), sliding tracks (usually 20–30% more than hinged per HomeLane), old unit removal.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: How is the wardrobe area measured? A: Width × height of the front face, in sq ft. A 6 ft wide, 7 ft high wardrobe is 42 sq ft. Lofts are added separately.
    • Q: Labour-only or with material — which is better? A: Labour-only lets you pick every brand yourself. With material is simpler. Either way, the quote shows both lines.
    • Q: Do sliding wardrobes cost more? A: Yes. Tracks and heavier hardware add cost; HomeLane puts it at about 20–30% over hinged.
    • Q: Which plywood suits Patna's humidity? A: BWR or BWP plywood holds up better to damp than MDF or particle board.
    • Q: How long does a new wardrobe take? A: Usually 5–10 working days on site for a standard bedroom wardrobe, depending on size and finish.
  • Price unit: job (repairs) / sq ft (new wardrobe)
  • Tags: wardrobe banwana, almirah repair, kabja badalna, drawer channel, cupboard lock, almari ka tala, wardrobe per sq ft rate Patna

Face Bleach (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: mens-face-bleach-at-home | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Mild face and neck bleach at home to lighten facial hair and even out dull skin tone.

The groomer does a patch test on your inner arm first, then cleans your face, mixes a mild bleach cream (oxy or protein type) and applies it on face and neck. It stays on for about 10–15 minutes, then is wiped off and a soothing cream is applied. It is quick and works well before an event. Not for broken, sunburnt or very sensitive skin.

De-Tan Treatment (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: mens-detan-at-home | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): A de-tan pack for face, neck or hands to reduce sun tan and dullness, done at home.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Patna summers and bike rides leave a tan line on the face, neck and arms. A de-tan pack helps lift that tan and brightens dull skin. The groomer cleans the skin, applies a de-tan pack (often with ingredients like yoghurt, papaya or lemon extracts), leaves it on for about 10–15 minutes, then removes it and applies a moisturiser. Unlike bleach, it does not lighten hair. Results show after the first session and improve with repeat sessions.
  • Bullets:
    • Cleansing before the pack
    • De-tan pack on face & neck or hands
    • Moisturiser after removal
    • Sealed, branded products
    • Can be added to a haircut or cleanup visit

    • Face + hands de-tan combo — 45 min — ₹549 [confidence medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: full back or legs de-tan (priced per area), facials.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: De-tan or bleach — which is better? A: De-tan works on sun tan without lightening hair. Bleach lightens facial hair too. For tan alone, choose de-tan.
    • Q: How many sessions? A: You see a change in one session. Heavy tan may need 2–3 sessions a couple of weeks apart.
    • Q: Is it safe for sensitive skin? A: Usually yes, but tell the groomer. Avoid it on cuts or sunburn.
    • Q: How do I keep the tan away? A: Use sunscreen daily and cover up on two-wheelers.

Face Scrub (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: mens-face-scrub-at-home | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): A quick face and neck scrub at home to clear dead skin, oil and dust. Best as an add-on.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): A scrub removes dead skin and surface dirt so your face feels smoother and shaves better. The groomer cleans your face, massages a gentle scrub over face and neck for a few minutes, wipes it off with warm towels and applies a light moisturiser. It takes about 15 minutes, so it works best as an add-on to a haircut or beard trim. For deeper results, choose a cleanup, which includes a scrub plus steam and a pack.
  • Bullets:
    • Face cleanse first
    • Gentle scrub massage on face and neck
    • Warm towel wipe-off and moisturiser
    • About 15 minutes
  • Packages:
    • Face & neck scrub (add-on) — 15 min — ₹149 [no standalone men's scrub price found on UC Patna/metro or tier-2 lists; scrub is part of cleanups priced ₹549–699; estimate [INFERENCE]; confidence low]
    • Scrub + 10 min face massage — 25 min — ₹249 [UC Patna face massage 10 min ₹149; confidence low-medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: steam, blackhead removal and face pack (book a cleanup instead).
  • FAQs:
    • Q: How often should I scrub? A: Once or twice a week at most. Over-scrubbing can irritate skin.
    • Q: Can I book only a scrub? A: It is best as an add-on with a haircut or beard trim, since the visit is short.
    • Q: I have acne. Is it okay? A: Avoid scrubbing over active pimples. The groomer will skip those areas.
    • Q: Scrub or cleanup? A: A scrub is quick surface care. A cleanup adds steam, blackhead removal and a pack.
  • Price unit: job
  • Tags/keywords: face scrub men, chehre ki ragdai, scrub karwana, dead skin hatana, face polish gents, scrub at home Patna
  • Image brief: Hero — groomer's hands massaging a light scrub on a man's cheek, warm towel nearby. Package photo — scrub tube and folded warm towels on a tray.
  • Sources: https://www.urbancompany.com/patna-mens-grooming — Patna 10-min face massage ₹149, cleanups (which include cleansing and dead-skin removal) ₹599–699; https://citystyle.co.in/rate-chart.php — tier-2 cleanup prices ₹549–649 (no standalone scrub); Yes Madam men's pages returned JS-only content, no prices captured.

Summary:

  • Items done: Haircut & Styling, Beard Trimming & Shave, Facial & Cleanup, Face Bleach, De-Tan Treatment, Face Scrub (all 6).

B. Women's Beauty: hair and face

Data note: Urban Company (UC) publishes city pages. UC Patna women's hair prices are the SAME as UC Bengaluru (looks like national pricing). UC Patna "Salon Prime" skin prices are about ₹100–250 lower than Bengaluru. Very little published price data exists for Patna local parlours, so the "Patna est." column mostly uses UC Patna plus a few local salon offers. All prices were checked on 2026-10-23.

The stylist checks your hair length and texture, then does the cut or trim. If you book styling, your hair is blow-dried straight, given soft in/out curls, or ironed for a smooth finish. Your hair should be washed and dry before the visit, unless you book a wash. The stylist cleans up loose hair afterwards. This works for a monthly trim, a new cut, or getting ready for a function.

Hair Straightening — Smoothening / Keratin / Botox (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: hair-smoothening-keratin | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Keratin, smoothening or hair botox at home for frizz-free, manageable hair. Price depends on hair length.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): These treatments reduce frizz and make hair easier to manage for weeks or months. Keratin and botox coat the hair and seal it with heat, so hair looks smoother and shinier without becoming poker-straight. Smoothening uses a stronger chemical step, so the hair becomes straighter and the change lasts longer. The stylist washes your hair, applies the product section by section, blow-dries it and then irons it in fine sections to seal the treatment. It takes 3–4 hours, so plan a free afternoon. Aftercare matters: use a sulphate-free shampoo and conditioner.
  • Bullets:
    • Hair-length check and strand test before starting
    • Wash, product application, blow-dry and ironing in fine sections to seal it in
    • Branded professional products (brand named on the booking)
    • Aftercare advice: sulphate-free shampoo and when to first wash
  • Packages:
    • Keratin — up to shoulder length — 180 min — ₹3,499 [Patna est. ₹3,499–3,999 (UC Patna "from ₹3,999"; Beauty Island Patna offer ₹3,499 any length, date unknown); metro ref from ₹3,999 (UC Bengaluru); medium]
    • Keratin — below shoulder / waist length — 210–240 min — ₹4,499 [Patna est. ₹4,000–5,000, inferred from UC "starts at" + length surcharges; low]
    • Smoothening — up to shoulder length — 210 min — ₹3,299 [no UC home listing found; in-salon refs: smoothening ₹2,999+ (Super Cuts, Dharamshala), ₹2,499 offer (Elight, Chennai); low]
    • Hair botox — any length up to mid-back — 180 min — ₹4,999 [Patna est. ₹5,000–5,500 (UC Patna ₹5,499); metro ref ₹5,499 (UC Bengaluru); medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: extra-long or very thick hair (surcharge set after the length check), haircut, sulphate-free aftercare shampoo and conditioner, a second product brand if the customer asks for one.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Keratin or smoothening, which should I pick? A: Keratin is gentler and keeps some natural movement. Smoothening makes hair straighter and lasts longer, but uses a stronger chemical. The stylist will suggest one after looking at your hair.
    • Q: How long does it last? A: It depends on the product, your hair, and how often you wash it. Ask the stylist for the product's expected life. Sulphate-free shampoo helps it last longer.
    • Q: When can I wash my hair? A: Follow what the stylist tells you for the product used. Usually you wait a few days. After that, use only sulphate-free shampoo.
    • Q: Is it safe on coloured hair? A: Usually yes. Tell the stylist about any colour, henna or past chemical treatment so they can do a strand test first.
    • Q: Why is the final price different from the listed price? A: Listed prices are for the length shown. Longer or thicker hair needs more product. The stylist confirms the price before starting.
  • Price unit: job (by hair length)
  • Tags/keywords: keratin at home Patna, hair smoothening price, baal seedhe karna, frizzy hair treatment, rebonding, hair botox, baal silky karna, keratin treatment ghar par
  • Image brief: Hero: a stylist ironing a thin section of long dark hair at home, product bowl and clips on a side table. Package: before/after-style side view of frizzy vs smooth hair (use a model or stock image you have rights to; no brand logos).
  • Sources: https://www.urbancompany.com/patna-women-hair-services — UC Patna keratin from ₹3,999, botox ₹5,499 (3 hrs), haircut+keratin/botox package ₹5,248 (3 hr 45 min); UC aftercare note recommends sulphate-free shampoo and ironing in fine sections; https://www.facebook.com/beautyislandpatna/videos/offer-in-hair-straightening-price-in-patna-at-beauty-island-bridal-makeup-in-pat/1149427226365527 — Patna salon keratin offer ₹3,499 any length (date unknown); https://supercutsdharamshala.com/pricing — tier-3 salon keratin ₹3,499+, smoothening ₹2,999+; https://www.elightsalon.com/ — smoothening offer ₹2,499, keratin ₹3,555 (Chennai salon).

Hair Coloring — Root Touch-up / Global / Highlights (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: hair-colour-at-home | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Root touch-up, full hair colour or highlights at home with L'Oréal professional shades, or your own colour.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): A trained stylist colours your hair at home. They help you pick a shade, check your scalp, and do a patch test if this is your first time with the brand. A root touch-up covers grey regrowth near the scalp. Global colour covers all your hair in one shade. Highlights or balayage add lighter strands for a softer look. The stylist uses L'Oréal Majirel or the ammonia-free Inoa range, applies it section by section, rinses, and leaves your hair conditioned. If you already have a colour you like, you can book application only.
  • Bullets:
    • Shade consultation, plus a patch test on request
    • Sealed L'Oréal Majirel or Inoa (ammonia-free) colour, or apply your own colour

  • FAQs:
    • Q: Majirel or Inoa, what's the difference? A: Both are L'Oréal Professionnel colours. Inoa has no ammonia, so it smells milder and feels gentler, and it costs a little more. Majirel gives strong grey coverage.
    • Q: Do I need a patch test? A: We recommend one if you are new to a brand or have a sensitive scalp. Book it a day or two before the colour visit.
    • Q: Can I use my own colour? A: Yes. Book "application only". The stylist will check that the pack is sealed and in date before using it.
    • Q: Will it stain my floor or towels? A: The stylist covers the area and your shoulders. Wear an old T-shirt, as colour can stain fabric.
    • Q: How often should I do a root touch-up? A: Most people need one every 4–6 weeks, depending on how fast their hair grows and how much grey shows.

  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Gentle face and neck bleach at home to lighten fine facial hair and even out skin tone.

The beautician cleans your face, does a quick patch test if you are new to the product, and mixes a small fresh batch. It is spread on the face and neck, left on for a few minutes, then wiped off and cooled with a soothing pack. We use face-safe bleach creams such as Oxylife or O3+. Full Body Waxing (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: full-body-waxing | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Full arms, underarms, legs, stomach and back waxed at home by a trained woman beautician.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): A woman beautician comes to your home with a fresh wax kit and disposable spatulas and strips. She waxes full arms, underarms, full legs, stomach and back in one sitting. Pick honey wax for normal skin or Rica / roll-on wax if your skin is sensitive or you want less pulling. Bikini and bikini line are not part of this package and can be added. Let hair grow 3–5 weeks first and do not shave in between. Plan about 90 minutes and keep a fan or AC running in the room.
  • Bullets (3–5, what the customer gets):
    • Woman beautician at your door with her own kit, sheet and wipes
    • Fresh spatula for each dip; no double-dipping in the wax pot
    • Choice of honey, Rica or chocolate roll-on wax
    • Post-wax soothing lotion/wipe on all waxed areas
  • Packages (2–4):
    • Full body – Honey wax — arms+underarms, full legs, stomach, back — 90 min — ₹1,299 [Patna est. ₹1,300–1,550 (UC from ₹1,519); metro ref ₹1,199–1,419 (Home Salon honey ₹1,199; UC Delhi from ₹1,419); confidence medium]
    • Full body – Rica wax — same areas, Rica strip wax — 100 min — ₹1,899 [Patna est. ₹1,800–2,200 (inferred: honey + ~50 %); metro ref ₹1,899–2,299 (Home Salon); confidence low-medium]
    • Full body – Rica/chocolate roll-on — same areas, roll-on cartridges — 100 min — ₹1,999 [Patna est. ₹1,600–2,200 (UC roll-on option exists, starts ₹1,519); metro ref ₹2,099–2,699 Home Salon roll-on body packages; confidence low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: bikini, bikini line, buttocks, face; threading; bleach/detan; travel beyond service radius (if applicable).
  • FAQs (4–6 Q&A, own words, answers ≤ 40 words):
    • Q: How long should my hair be? A: About 3–5 weeks of growth works best. Very short hair is harder to pull and hurts more. Do not trim or shave before the visit.
    • Q: Rica or honey — which should I choose? A: Honey suits most skin and costs less. Rica is gentler and better for sensitive or dry skin, so it costs more.
    • Q: Is bikini waxing included? A: No. Bikini and bikini line are separate add-ons with peel-off wax.
    • Q: Can I get waxed while pregnant? A: Usually yes, but tell the beautician first. Skin is more sensitive then; check with your doctor if unsure.
    • Q: What should I arrange at home? A: A cool room (fan/AC), a chair or bed with space around it, and a bathroom to wash up afterwards.
    • Q: How often should I wax? A: Most people repeat every 4–6 weeks. First-timers often go every 2–3 weeks for a few rounds.
  • Price unit: visit (per package)
  • Tags/keywords: full body wax at home Patna, ghar par waxing, ladies beautician at home, rica wax Patna, parlour wali ghar pe, body wax ladies, waxing ghar par Patna
  • Image brief: Hero — beautician in apron applying wax with spatula on a woman's forearm in a bright home bedroom, kit tray beside. Package photo — flat-lay of wax heater, spatulas, strips, gloves on a clean towel.

Half Body Waxing (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: half-body-waxing | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Full arms, underarms and full legs waxed at home — the most-booked waxing combo.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): This covers the areas most women wax every month: full arms, underarms and full legs. The beautician brings her own wax heater, disposable spatulas and strips, and finishes with a soothing wipe. Choose honey wax for a lower price, Rica for sensitive skin, or chocolate roll-on for a faster, less messy session. Underarms are done with peel-off wax, which grips short hair and is easier on thin skin. Stomach, back and bikini can be added. Expect about one hour.
  • Bullets:
    • Full arms + underarms + full legs in one visit
    • Peel-off wax for underarms
    • Honey, Rica or roll-on — your choice
    • Add-ons: stomach, back, bikini line, face
  • Packages:
    • Honey wax — arms+underarms+full legs — 60 min — ₹549 [Patna est. ₹550–650 (UC spatula combo from ₹599); metro ref ₹529–729 (Home Salon honey pack, incl. threading), UC Delhi from ₹699; confidence medium-high]
    • Chocolate roll-on — same — 60 min — ₹699 [Patna est. ₹700–800 (UC roll-on from ₹749); metro ref ₹879 Yes Madam Rica Gold roll-on; confidence medium]
    • Rica wax — same — 65 min — ₹849 [Patna est. ₹800–1,000 (inferred); metro ref ₹849–949 (Home Salon Rica pack), ₹1,899 Rica arms+legs bundle elsewhere on Home Salon; confidence low-medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: stomach, back, bikini/bikini line, face, threading.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Are underarms included? A: Yes, full arms here include underarms, done with peel-off wax.
    • Q: What does roll-on mean? A: Wax comes in a heated cartridge rolled onto skin. It spreads a thin, even layer and is less messy than pot wax.
    • Q: Will it remove tan? A: Waxing lifts a thin top layer of skin, so mild tan often looks lighter. It is not a detan treatment.
    • Q: Can I bathe after waxing? A: Use lukewarm water and skip soap scrubs, sun and swimming for about a day.
    • Q: I shaved last week. Can I still book? A: Better to wait 2–3 more weeks. Short, stubbly hair is hard to grip.
  • Price unit: visit

Leg Waxing (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: leg-waxing | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Half or full leg waxing at home with honey or Rica wax.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Book only your legs when that is all you need. Half legs means toe to knee; full legs goes from toe to the top of the thigh but does not include the bikini line. The beautician cleans the skin, applies wax in sections, removes it with strips and finishes with a calming wipe. Honey wax is the budget choice; Rica is kinder to dry or sensitive skin. A half-leg session takes about 20 minutes and full legs about 30.
  • Bullets:

    • Honey or Rica wax

  • FAQs:
    • Q: Where does half legs stop? A: At the knee. Above the knee is part of full legs.
    • Q: Is bikini line included in full legs? A: No, it is a separate add-on.
    • Q: Does waxing make hair grow back thicker? A: No. Waxing removes hair from the root, so regrowth is usually finer. Shaving is what makes it feel coarse.
    • Q: My skin goes red after waxing. Is that normal? A: Mild redness or small bumps usually settle within a few hours. Avoid hot water, sun and tight clothes that day.

Face Waxing (category: Home Salon & Beauty)

  • Suggested slug: face-waxing | Mode: doorstep | Status: Coming soon
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Peel-off wax for upper lip, chin, sidelocks, forehead or full face, done gently at home.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Facial skin is thin, so face waxing uses peel-off (hard) wax. It sets on the hair and lifts off without a strip, which pulls the skin less and catches fine hair. Book a single area like upper lip or chin, or the full face (forehead, sidelocks, upper lip, chin, jawline and neck if chosen). Eyebrows are not waxed — eyebrow shaping is done by threading and booked separately. A full face takes about 30 minutes.
  • Bullets:
    • Peel-off wax made for the face
    • Single areas or full face
    • Fresh spatula for every area
    • Calming wipe afterwards
  • Packages:
    • Single area (upper lip / chin / forehead) — 10 min — ₹79 [Patna est. ₹80–100 (UC peel-off face waxing from ₹89); metro ref ₹55–120 per area (Home Salon Rica Brazilian); confidence medium]
    • Upper lip + chin — 15 min — ₹149 [Patna est. ₹150–180 (inferred from UC per-area start); confidence low-medium]
    • Full face — forehead, sidelocks, upper lip, chin, jawline — 30 min — ₹299 [Patna est. ₹280–380 (inferred; UC Patna full-face price not shown on web); metro ref ₹399 Home Salon full face Rica Brazilian; confidence low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: eyebrow threading; neck waxing (add-on); bleach/detan.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Can you wax my eyebrows? A: No. We shape eyebrows with threading, which gives better control. Add threading to the same visit.
    • Q: Is face waxing safe? A: Yes when done with peel-off wax by a trained beautician. Skip it if you have active acne, sunburn or cuts on the face.
    • Q: I use retinol or acne creams. Is that a problem? A: Stop retinoids a few days before waxing and tell the beautician; they can make skin peel or tear.
    • Q: Waxing or threading for upper lip? A: Threading suits very sensitive skin; waxing is quicker and removes fine hair too. Choose what feels better for you.

Pick an HD look for a soft, camera-friendly finish, or airbrush for a lighter feel that holds through long rituals. Every package covers skin prep, full face makeup, lashes, a bridal hairstyle, and draping of the lehenga or saree with dupatta setting. A paid trial is available a few weeks before the wedding and the trial fee is adjusted if you book. The artist plans time around the baraat and jaimala.
  • Bullets:
    • Skin prep, primer, full HD or airbrush face makeup and long-wear setting
    • Strip lashes included; eye look matched to outfit
    • Bridal hairstyle (bun or open style), placement of maang tikka, matha patti and dupatta
    • Lehenga / saree draping with double-dupatta setting
    • Paid trial, adjusted against the final booking
  • Packages:
    • Bridal Trial — one look tested on half or full face, hairstyle idea discussed — 90 min — ₹1,999, adjusted if bridal package booked [Patna: WedMeGood listings show "paid trial – money adjusted if booked" as common; no price published; confidence low]
    • Classic Bridal — regular long-wear makeup, lashes, bridal hairdo, draping — 180 min — ₹6,999 [Patna est. ₹5,000–8,000 (WMG starting prices ₹5,000–₹8,000 for several Patna artists); metro ref ₹4,999–8,000 entry level (Delhi, Bengaluru WMG); confidence medium]
    • HD Bridal — HD makeup, lashes, bridal hairdo with accessory setting, draping — 210 min — ₹9,999 [Patna est. ₹8,000–12,000 (WMG Patna cluster ₹7,000–₹12,000); metro ref ₹15,000–20,000 mid-range (Bengaluru/Delhi WMG); confidence medium]
    • Airbrush Bridal — airbrush base, lashes, premium hairdo, draping, touch-up kit left with bride — 240 min — ₹14,999 [Patna est. ₹14,000–18,000 (Glamup by Ujjwala airbrush ₹18,000); metro ref ₹14,000–25,000 (Bride Factory Bengaluru ₹14,000; Delhi ₹7,999–₹50,000); confidence medium-low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: hair extensions and fresh flowers; nail extensions/mehndi; pre-bridal skin and body services (separate packages); family members' makeup (book Party Makeup); early-morning slot before 6 am (propose ₹500); travel outside Patna city (Patna artists list outstation charges ₹11,000–₹15,000 per function, e.g. Glamup by Ujjwala); stay-back for touch-ups beyond 1 hour.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: HD or airbrush — which should I choose? A: HD gives a soft, natural finish and costs less. Airbrush is sprayed on, feels lighter and lasts longer in heat and sweat. For summer weddings or very long ceremonies, airbrush is a safer pick.
    • Q: Is a trial necessary? A: Not required, but helpful. It lets you check shade match, eye look and hairstyle before the day. The trial fee is adjusted if you book the bridal package.
    • Q: Will the artist come to the marriage hall or hotel? A: Yes, anywhere within Patna city at no extra charge. Places outside Patna have a travel charge shown before you confirm.
    • Q: How long does bridal makeup take? A: Plan three to four hours including hair and draping. The artist will give a start time based on your baraat or jaimala time.
    • Q: Do I need to pay an advance? A: Yes, an advance blocks the date. Many Patna artists ask for about 50% (seen on WedMeGood). Pay the rest online or in cash after the service.
    • Q: What should I keep ready? A: Clean, moisturised face, washed dry hair, jewellery, safety pins, outfit and dupatta. Share outfit photos and reference looks a few days before.
  • Price unit: event (per bride, per function)
  • Tags/keywords: bridal makeup Patna, dulhan makeup, shaadi makeup at home, HD bridal makeup, airbrush makeup Bihar, dulhan ka makeup ghar pe, lehenga draping, bridal juda
  • Image brief: Hero — Indian bride in red lehenga, seated, artist fixing maang tikka and dupatta, soft indoor light; no visible brand logos. Package photo — airbrush gun and HD kit laid out next to a bridal hair bun with flowers.
  • Sources: https://www.wedmegood.com/vendors/patna/bridal-makeup/ — Patna starting bridal prices (₹5,000 Beauty Canvas, ₹6,000 Aastha, ₹7,000 Koral/V2L/Head Masters/Beauty Island, ₹8,000 Rupa, ₹10,000 Lakme Salon Dakbunglow/SC Glamour/Mamta Sinngh, ₹11,000 Riya Roshan/Glamup, ₹12,000 Deeksha/Pallavi, ₹15,000 Akriti/Diksha, ₹19,500 Beauty Buzz) and trial policies (paid trial adjusted if booked / free trial / no trial); https://www.wedmegood.com/profile/Glamup-by-Ujjwala-3780275 — Patna airbrush ₹18,000, outstation ₹11,000–15,000 per function, 50% advance, lashes and hairstyling offered; https://www.wedmegood.com/profile/Bride-Factory-Bangalore-498471 — Bengaluru bridal ₹8,000, airbrush ₹14,000; https://www.wedmegood.com/profile/the-makeups-hub-4123638 — Delhi bridal ₹4,999, airbrush ₹7,999; https://www.wedmegood.com/profile/VS-Makeovers-507656 — Delhi top end bridal ₹45,000, airbrush ₹50,000; https://www.wedmegood.com/profile/Makeup-by-Divya-Nagaraj-1094226 — Bengaluru bridal ₹15,000, airbrush ₹20,000; https://www.wedmegood.com/profile/Makeup-by-Nimmy-Jagath-469956 — bridal packages commonly list hair styling, draping, makeup, false lashes.

6. Home Tuition

Cross-cutting notes for the owner (apply to all 4 items)

  • Patna bureaus (Mahavir, Universal, Anupama, Krishna, Gyan Setu) do NOT publish monthly fees; they quote after hearing class, board, subjects and days/week. The only Patna rate card found is Royal Home Tuition (per-hour "onwards" floors, page ~3+ years old). Monthly figures below are derived = hourly rate × sessions, cross-checked with UrbanPro Patna tutor-pay figures and TutorGali national monthly medians.
  • Norm in Patna: 1-to-1, tutor comes home, 3 days/week (subject tutor) or 5–6 days/week (all-subjects tutor), 1–1.5 hours per visit. Fee paid monthly, in advance or at month start.
  • Free demo is universal: 1 free demo class (Mahavir, Universal, Anupama, UrbanPro) and some give a 2-day free trial (Saraswati). Recommend TiptoBook: 1 free demo visit, then pay monthly.
  • Bureau business model: Universal Home Tuition takes 50% of the first month's fee as its commission; tutors also pay registration (Universal ₹1,000/yr; Mahavir ₹499). TiptoBook can undercut by charging parents a flat monthly fee with no "first month half" surprise.
  • Verification claims seen: ID check, qualification check, experience screening (Universal); "background-checked" (Mahavir, Anupama). TuitionsInIndia openly says it does NOT verify tutors. TiptoBook should state only what it actually does (e.g., Aadhaar/ID + qualification certificate check + in-person interview) — do not claim police verification unless done.
  • Online option: national data shows online is NOT much cheaper per hour (TuitionsInIndia: ~₹500/hr typical both ways); TutorGali shows online primary median ₹3,000 vs ₹4,000 at home. Suggest online = ~20% below home-visit price.
  • Female tutor on request is a common selling point in Patna (Mahavir, Anupama). Replacement tutor if not satisfied is common (Universal: "fast tutor replacement").
