AC Gas Refilling & Leak Fix (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: ac-gas-refill | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description: Leak check, leak repair and full gas refill for split and window ACs, all common gas types.
  • Full description: If your AC runs but the air is not cold, or ice forms on the pipes, gas may have leaked. Our technician first finds the leak using pressure and soap tests, fixes it, and then vacuums the line and refills the correct gas for your model (R32, R410A or R22). Refilling without fixing the leak wastes money, so we always check first. After the refill, the technician measures pressure and cooling at the vent. You will see the price of any part before work starts.
  • Bullets:
    • Leak detection on indoor unit, outdoor unit and pipe joints
    • Minor leak fix (flare nut re-tightening/brazing at joints) included
    • Full gas refill as per AC model and tonnage
    • Pressure and vent-temperature check after refill
  • Packages:
    • Gas refill — split or window, up to 1.5 ton — leak check + minor joint fix + full refill — 150 min — ₹2,699 [Patna est. ₹3,000–3,499 (UC ₹3,000; ACI ₹3,499); metro ref ₹3,000 (UC Delhi); market ₹2,000–4,500 (NB); confidence high]
    • Gas refill — 2 ton split — same as above — 150 min — ₹2,999 [ACI charges same ₹3,499 for 1/1.5/2 ton; UC flat ₹3,000; confidence medium — bigger units use more gas]
    • Gas top-up (partial, pressure low but no leak found) — 60 min — ₹1,499 [no direct source; TiptoBook proposal; confidence low]
    • Leak check only (no refill) — 45 min — ₹349 [UC AC repair check-up starts ₹299; confidence medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: coil/condenser repair (UC ₹899), copper coil replacement (UC ₹4,000–6,500), compressor, capillary/filter (UC ₹350), flare nut (UC ₹150), outdoor stand. Gas type pricing: no public source showed different prices for R32/R410A/R22 — UC and ACI quote one flat price.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: How do I know my AC needs gas? A: Weak cooling even after cleaning, outdoor fan running but little cold air, or frost on the pipes. The technician confirms with a pressure gauge before refilling.
    • Q: Does gas finish over time? A: Not normally. Gas runs in a closed loop, so low gas usually means a leak. That is why we check for leaks first.
    • Q: Which gas will you fill? A: The same gas your AC was built for — R32, R410A or R22. It is written on the outdoor unit sticker. Mixing gases damages the AC.
    • Q: What if the leak is in the coil? A: The technician shows you the leak and gives a written price for coil repair or replacement before any work.
    • Q: Is there a warranty? A: Proposed: 30 days on gas and leak fix at the same joint (UC offers 30 days on gas refill).
  • Price unit: job
  • Tags/keywords: AC gas refill, AC gas filling, AC gas leak repair, AC thanda nahi kar raha, AC gas bharna, AC gas bharwana Patna, R32 gas refill, split AC gas charging
  • Image brief: Hero — technician at an outdoor AC unit on a balcony with a manifold pressure gauge connected, refrigerant cylinder beside. Package photo — close-up of gauge dial and brass service valve.
  • Sources: [UCP] — Patna gas refill & check-up ₹3,000, 2 hr 30 min; coil repair ₹899, flare nut ₹150, capillary ₹350, copper coils ₹4,000–6,500; repair check-up from ₹299; 30-day warranty on gas refill; [UCD] — Delhi same ₹3,000; [ACI] — Patna gas filling ₹3,499 for window and split 1/1.5/2 ton, 90-day warranty claim; [NB] — gas ₹2,000–4,500; [UCA] — market range ₹2,000–5,000.

For a window AC, the unit is fixed in the window frame and sealed. AC Uninstallation (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: ac-uninstallation | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description: Safe removal of split or window AC, with gas saved in the outdoor unit, ready for shifting.
  • Full description: Moving house or replacing an old AC? Our technician removes the unit without losing gas where the AC allows. For split ACs, gas is first pumped into the outdoor unit, then pipes are disconnected and capped, and both units are taken down. Window ACs are released from the frame and power is disconnected. Pipes and wires are coiled and packed with the unit, and the work area is cleaned. If you need the AC fitted at the new place, book installation or the shifting combo.

You get a clear quote listing parts and labour separately. Nothing is fixed until you approve. If you go ahead with the repair, the visit charge is adjusted in the final bill. If you decline, you pay only the visit charge. Works for semi-automatic, top-load and front-load machines of all common brands.

  • Excludes / charged extra: spare parts (drain pump, motor, PCB, door lock ₹1,100–1,400, gasket ₹1,300–1,600 — see table); PCB replacement on quote (₹2,500–6,000 per Haier-blog); descaling chemical if not in package; uninstall/re-install; stand/trolley; Bosch/Siemens jet service may be excluded (UC excludes them).
  • FAQs:
    • Q: My machine shows an error code. Can you fix it? A: Yes. The technician reads the code, tests the related part and quotes the fix before starting.
    • Q: Do you carry spare parts? A: Common parts yes. Brand-specific parts may take 1–3 days. You can also buy the part yourself.
    • Q: Can the control board be repaired instead of replaced? A: Often yes. Component-level repair usually costs much less than a new board. The technician will suggest which is better.
    • Q: Why is there loud noise during spin? A: Usually worn drum bearings, loose shock absorbers or an uneven load. Bearings need drum dismantling and take a few hours.
    • Q: Why does my machine smell? A: Detergent residue and dirt build up in the gasket and filter. A jet cleaning service removes it.
    • Q: Is there a warranty? A: Yes, 30 days on the repair work. Part warranty follows the part maker's terms.

Inverter Refrigerator Service (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: inverter-refrigerator-service | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description: Repair for inverter-compressor fridges: board, compressor and gas work by trained technicians.
  • Full description: Inverter fridges use a variable-speed compressor run by an electronic board. When cooling stops, the fault may be the board, the compressor or gas. Our technician checks voltage, reads error codes, tests the board and compressor separately, and checks gas pressure, so you only replace what has failed. Board repair is offered before replacement where possible. Most inverter fridges use R600a gas, which needs careful handling; our technicians follow safe charging steps.
  • Bullets:
    • Board and compressor tested separately
    • Board repair offered before replacement
    • Safe R600a gas charging with leak test
    • Fixed prices for board repair and gas
    • Proposed 90-day warranty on gas, compressor and board work
  • Packages:
    • Inverter check-up — diagnosis, error codes, voltage check — 60 min — ₹199 [Patna est. ₹199 UC-Patna; metro ₹199 UC-Delhi "double door (inverter)"; high]
    • Inverter PCB repair — 120 min — ₹1,999; PCB replacement from ₹2,999 [metro ₹2,200 repair / ₹3,200 replace NB-BLR; Patna none; low-medium]
    • Gas charging (inverter) — 150 min — ₹1,899 [Patna est. ₹1,825–2,325 SEVA-Patna; metro ₹1,800 NB-BLR; medium]
    • Inverter compressor replacement — compressor, brazing, gas — 240 min — from ₹7,499 [metro ₹6,400 part NB-BLR; brand centres ₹6,500–16,000 RESQ; low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: brand-original compressor/board price difference, stabiliser, workshop transport.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: How do I know my fridge is inverter? A: Check the label inside or the box; it often says "Inverter" or "Smart Inverter". The technician can confirm.
    • Q: Can the board be repaired instead of replaced? A: Often yes, and it costs less. We try repair first and tell you if replacement is needed.
    • Q: Does an inverter fridge need a stabiliser? A: Many have a wide voltage range, but in areas with heavy fluctuation a stabiliser helps protect the board.
    • Q: Is the compressor covered by brand warranty? A: Many brands give long compressor warranties. Check your card first; we'll tell you if it looks like a brand claim.
    • Q: What gas do you use? A: The same gas written on your fridge label, usually R600a or R134a.
  • Price unit: job
  • Tags: inverter fridge repair Patna, inverter compressor fridge, fridge board repair, fridge PCB kharab, smart inverter fridge not cooling, fridge compressor chal nahi raha
  • Image brief: Hero — technician with multimeter at the back panel of a fridge showing the compressor and inverter board. Package — clean shot of a fridge inverter PCB on a workbench.

TV Diagnosis & Troubleshooting (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: tv-diagnosis-repair-visit | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Technician visits, finds the fault in your LED/Smart TV and gives a written quote before any repair.

Small fixes such as loose cables, input/source settings, remote pairing, Wi-Fi or app problems and software reset are done in the same visit. If the repair needs a board, backlight or panel part, the visit fee is adjusted in the final repair bill. We service LED, LCD, Smart, QLED and OLED TVs of all major brands. CRT (old box) TVs are not covered.

    • Q: Do you repair old CRT (box) TVs? A: No. We service flat-panel TVs only: LED, LCD, Smart, QLED and OLED.
    • Q: Can a cracked screen be fixed? A: A cracked panel cannot be repaired, only replaced. Panel cost is often close to a new TV, so the technician will tell you honestly if replacing the TV makes more sense.

    • Q: My TV is still under brand warranty. Should I book? A: Contact the brand service centre first. Opening the TV through an outside technician may void the brand warranty.
    • Q: Should I repair an old TV? A: If the TV is over 8–10 years old or the repair is close to a new TV's price, we will suggest replacement.

  • Suggested slug: tv-backlight-repair | Mode: doorstep (panel opened at home or at workshop) | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Fix for dark screen with sound, dim or flickering picture by replacing failed LED backlight strips.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): If your TV has sound but a black or very dim picture, or the screen flickers or shows dark patches, the LED backlight strips are often at fault. Our technician first confirms the fault with a torch test and strip tester. The panel is then opened carefully in a dust-free area and all strips of the set are replaced, not just the dead one, so the brightness stays even. Price depends on screen size and brand. Cracked panels, vertical lines and T-CON faults are not backlight issues and are quoted separately.
  • Bullets:

  • FAQs:
    • Q: How do I know it's a backlight problem? A: If you hear sound and see a faint picture when shining a torch close to the screen, the backlight has most likely failed.

    • Q: Is it worth repairing? A: Backlight repair usually costs far less than a new panel. For TVs over 8 years old, we will tell you if replacement makes more sense.

Motherboard & Audio IC Repair (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: tv-motherboard-audio-repair | Mode: doorstep + workshop (bench repair) | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Chip-level repair or replacement of TV main board, power board and sound IC for no-power, restart and no-sound faults.

Our technician tests the boards at home. Where possible we repair at component level (capacitor, IC, regulator, software re-flash), which costs much less than a new board. If the board cannot be repaired, we quote a tested replacement board for your model. Faulty speakers, HDMI and USB ports are also handled. You approve the price before any work.

Geyser Check-up & Descaling (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: geyser-checkup-descaling | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Technician visits, finds why your geyser is not heating or tripping, and cleans out scale for faster heating.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Hard water in Patna leaves white scale inside geyser tanks and on the heating element. Over time the geyser takes longer to heat, uses more power, and may trip the MCB. Our technician first checks power supply, element, thermostat, wiring and earthing. For a full service, the tank is drained, the element is taken out and descaled, the tank is flushed, and the outer body is cleaned. You get a clear quote before any part is replaced. We work on electric storage and instant geysers of all common brands. Gas geysers are not covered.
  • Bullets:
    • Fault diagnosis: power, element, thermostat, wiring, earthing check

  • Packages:
    • Geyser check-up (diagnosis only) — inspection, fault finding, quote; fee adjusted in final repair bill — 30–45 min — ₹199 [Patna est. ₹0–249 (SevaMart free inspection [SM]; UC ₹249 [UCP]); metro ref ₹155–249 (NB ₹155 [NB], UC ₹249 national [UCP]; UC Jaipur ₹149 [UCJ]); confidence high — live Patna prices from two platforms]

    • Storage geyser service (10–25 L) — drain, remove and descale element, flush tank, check anode/thermostat, clean body, test — 60–75 min — ₹549 [Patna est. ₹599–799 [SM][UCP]; metro ref ₹475–599+ [NB][UCH]; confidence medium-high]

  • FAQs:
    • Q: How often should I get my geyser serviced? A: Once a year, ideally before winter (October–November). In hard-water areas, scale builds faster, so yearly descaling keeps heating fast and power use lower.
    • Q: Is the check-up fee extra if I get the repair done? A: No. The check-up fee is adjusted in the final repair bill if you approve the repair.
    • Q: Do you service gas geysers? A: No. We currently handle electric storage and instant geysers only.
    • Q: Will my geyser be unusable during service? A: Yes, for about an hour. Power and water supply to the geyser are switched off while the tank is drained and cleaned.
    • Q: My geyser is under brand warranty. Should I book? A: Check with the brand first. Racold, for example, gives multi-year cover on element and tank. Our service may affect brand warranty claims for internal parts.
  • Price unit: visit (check-up); job per geyser (service)

Heating Element & Thermostat Replacement (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: geyser-element-thermostat-replacement | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Geyser not heating, overheating, or tripping power? We test and replace the faulty element or thermostat.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Most geyser faults come from two parts. A burnt or scaled heating element means no hot water or a tripping MCB. A faulty thermostat means water stays cold, gets too hot, or the cut-off keeps triggering. Our technician tests both with a meter, shows you the faulty part, and quotes the part price before replacing it. The labour charge below covers removal, fitting a new gasket, refilling, and a heating test. Parts are billed at the listed rate, with local and branded options where available.
  • Bullets:
    • Meter test of element, thermostat and safety cut-out

    • New gasket fitted, tank refilled, leak and heating test
    • Old part handed back on request

  • FAQs:
    • Q: How do I know if it is the element or the thermostat? A: No heat and MCB trips usually means element. Water too hot, too cold, or light on with no heating often points to thermostat. The technician confirms by meter test.

    • Q: My geyser is 8–10 years old. Repair or replace? A: If the tank is leaking or rusted, replace. If only the element or thermostat failed and the tank is sound, repair is usually cheaper.

    • Q: Is my geyser's brand warranty still valid? A: Many brands cover the element for several years (Racold: 3–4 years on some models [RAC]). Check your warranty card first; the brand may replace it free.

Geyser Installation & Uninstallation (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: geyser-installation-uninstallation | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Wall-mounting and plumbing hookup for new instant or storage geysers, plus safe removal of old units.

Our technician drills and mounts the geyser on a solid wall, connects inlet and outlet pipes, fits the safety or pressure valve, connects power to an earthed socket, fills the tank, and runs a heating and leak test. Storage geysers (10–25 L) are heavier and need stronger anchoring than small instant units (1–6 L). For removal, we isolate power and water, drain the tank, detach the unit and cap the pipes. Pipes, valves and wiring are billed separately if needed.
  • Bullets:
    • Wall mounting with the brand's bracket and anchors

    • Earthing check and plug-point connection test
    • Fill, heat and leak test before we leave
    • Uninstall: drain, detach, cap pipes safely

    • Storage geyser installation (10–25 L) — mount on load-bearing wall, connect, safety valve, test — 60 min — ₹449 [Patna est. ₹499 [SM][UCP]; metro ref ₹386–499 [NB][NBH][UCB]; confidence high]

  • FAQs:
    • Q: Does the brand install free? A: Some brands include free installation with a new geyser. Check your invoice. If not, or if you want it sooner, book us.
    • Q: Do I need to buy pipes and valves? A: New geysers usually come with a bracket and safety valve. Pipes are often extra. The technician will tell you on arrival and bill only what is used.
    • Q: Instant or storage — which needs more work? A: Storage geysers are heavier when full and need a strong wall and good anchoring. Instant geysers are smaller and quicker to mount.
    • Q: What power point is needed? A: A dedicated 15/16 A earthed socket for storage geysers. Instant geysers (3 kW) also need a 15/16 A point. We do not run new wiring under this service.
    • Q: Can you install on a hollow or weak wall? A: No. For safety, storage geysers go on a solid brick or concrete wall only.

Tank Leakage & Valve Repair (category: AC & Home Appliance)

  • Suggested slug: geyser-leakage-valve-repair | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Water dripping from your geyser? We fix leaking valves, pipes and gaskets, and check if the tank is damaged.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): A dripping geyser wastes water and can be unsafe near live wiring. Most leaks come from a worn safety or pressure valve, loose inlet/outlet pipes, or an old gasket around the element flange. Our technician switches off power, finds the leak source, and replaces the worn part. If the inner tank itself is cracked or rusted through, we tell you honestly: tank replacement costs a large share of a new geyser, and replacing the unit may make more sense. Constant dripping from the valve can also mean high water pressure, which we check.
  • Bullets:
    • Leak source traced: valve, pipe joint, gasket or tank
    • Safety/pressure valve and pipe joint replacement
    • Flange gasket replacement with tank drain and refill
    • Honest repair-vs-replace advice for tank leaks
    • Leak test before we leave
  • Packages:
    • Valve or pipe leak fix — replace safety/pressure/multi-function valve or inlet/outlet pipe, reseal joints — 30–45 min — ₹249 + part [Patna est. ₹499 repair labour [SM]; metro ref ₹155–249 visit + parts [NB][UCP]; confidence medium]
    • Gasket leak fix (flange) — drain tank, remove element, fit new gasket, refill, test — 60 min — ₹399 + gasket [Patna est. ₹499 [SM]; confidence medium — labour similar to element swap]
    • Inner tank replacement — remove old tank, fit new copper/SS tank, reassemble, test — 120–180 min — ₹999 + tank [Patna est.: no listing; parts ₹1,800–4,000 [NB]; confidence low — rarely done; owner may prefer "quote on inspection"]
  • Part price guide [NB]: safety valve ₹300; pressure release valve ₹400; multi-function valve ₹400; angle valve ₹400; SS inlet/outlet pipe ₹250 (18") / ₹300 (36"); rubber washer/O-ring ₹50; gasket ₹150–400 by size; SS/brass assembly ₹1,200; copper tank ₹1,800 (<10 L) / ₹3,000 (10–30 L) / ₹4,000 (30–50 L); SS tank ₹1,800 / ₹2,400 / ₹3,200.
  • Excludes / charged extra: all parts above, bathroom plumbing outside the geyser (book plumber), pressure-reducing valve for the house line, wall/tile repair from past leaks, gas geysers.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Water drips from the small valve when the geyser heats. Is that normal? A: A few drops while heating can be normal; the valve releases pressure. A steady flow or drip when the geyser is off means the valve needs replacing.
    • Q: Can a leaking tank be welded? A: We do not weld tanks; it is not safe for a pressurised heater. We replace the tank or advise buying a new geyser.
    • Q: Is a leaking geyser dangerous? A: It can be if water reaches the wiring. Switch off the geyser at the MCB or socket and close the inlet valve until the technician arrives.
    • Q: Is tank replacement worth it? A: Compare costs. A new tank plus labour can approach the price of a small new geyser (Havells 3 L instant lists at ₹3,790 [HAV]). Many brands give 5–7 years tank warranty, so check that first.
  • Price unit: job (labour) + parts at rate card
  • Tags/keywords: geyser leakage, geyser se pani tapak raha, geyser valve change, geyser tank leak, safety valve repair, geyser pipe leak, geyser rubber badalna, water heater leaking Patna
  • Image brief: Hero — close-up of technician's gloved hands fitting a brass safety valve under a wall geyser. Package photo — new safety valve, braided SS pipes and rubber gasket laid out on a towel.
  • Sources: [NB] valve, pipe, gasket and tank prices; [SM] Patna repair ₹499; [UCP] ₹249 visit adjusted into repair; [HAV] instant geyser price for repair-vs-replace; [RAC] Racold 7-year tank warranty on listed models.

    • Plan + 3D elevation — 2D plan + 3D front elevation, up to 3 minor revisions — PROPOSED ₹7,999 (G+1) [Bihar est.: ArchetiQ Basic plan ₹8,999, 3D elevation ₹4,500–9,000 first floor + ₹1,000 each extra floor; confidence medium]
    • Complete drawing set — 2D plans, 3D elevation, working drawing, footing layout, structural + MEP drawings — PROPOSED ₹13,999 up to ~1,500 sq ft, else ₹20/sq ft [Bihar est.: ArchetiQ Standard ₹14,999; structural ₹10.8/sq ft, MEP ₹9/sq ft; metro ref architect fees ₹20–150/sq ft (aradhanakakardesigns); confidence medium]
    • 3D interior render — per room view — PROPOSED ₹1,499/room [no source checked; confidence low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: municipal map sanction fees and approval liaison, soil test, site visits outside Patna, major redesign after approval, printed sheets, GST.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: What do you need from me? A: Plot length and width, road side and direction, number of floors, and the rooms you want. A rough sketch or photo of the plot helps.
    • Q: Is the plan Vastu-compliant? A: If you ask, rooms are placed following common Vastu guidance. Tell us your priorities, like kitchen or pooja room direction.
    • Q: How many changes can I ask for? A: Minor revisions (up to three is common) are included. Big layout changes after approval are charged.
    • Q: Will this plan get municipal approval? A: Drawings follow standard setbacks, but sanction depends on Patna Municipal Corporation/authority rules. Approval fees and liaison are separate.
    • Q: Can my contractor build from this? A: The floor plan alone is for layout. For building, take the complete set with working, structural and electrical/plumbing drawings.
  • Price unit: sq ft (plans) / floor (elevation) / job (bundles)
  • Tags/keywords: ghar ka naksha, house map Patna, naksha banwana, 3D elevation Patna, front design ghar, vastu naksha, makan ka plan, structural drawing Bihar
  • Image brief: Hero — architect's desk with printed 2D floor plan and tablet showing a 3D house elevation. Package photo — clean 3D render of a modern two-storey Indian house front.

False Ceiling & Lighting (category: Construction & Interior Design)

  • Suggested slug: false-ceiling-lighting | Mode: visit (measurement) then doorstep install | Status: live
  • Short description: Gypsum, POP or PVC false ceilings with cove and panel lights, priced per square foot.
  • Full description: A technician visits to measure the room, check ceiling height and discuss the design and lights you want. Choose gypsum board for a smooth, quick finish, POP for detailed shapes, or PVC panels for bathrooms, balconies and damp areas. The price covers the frame, boards, joint finishing and primer, with light cut-outs. LED panels, cove strips and wiring for them are quoted per point. Most single rooms are finished within a few days, and debris is cleared after work.
  • Bullets:
    • Free measurement and design discussion
    • Gypsum, POP or PVC options, simple or cove designs
    • GI channel frame, boards, joint taping and primer
    • Cut-outs and wiring for LED panels, spot lights and cove strips (lights extra)
    • Site cleaned after work
  • Packages:
    • PVC panel ceiling — PVC panels on frame, for bathroom/balcony/kitchen — ~1 day per room — PROPOSED ₹95/sq ft [Patna est.: no PVC-specific Patna rate found; metro ref Livspace laminate PVC ₹160–185 + GST; indiframe/advancelam India-wide lower; confidence low]
    • POP ceiling — POP on GI frame, plain or simple border — 3–5 days per room — PROPOSED ₹85/sq ft [Tier-2 est.: POP ₹85/sq ft (aajjo, Varanasi seller); indiframe 10x10 POP ₹6,000–12,000 (₹60–120/sq ft); metro ref Livspace ₹95–105 + GST; confidence medium]
    • Gypsum ceiling (standard) — Gyproc-type board, GI channels, taping, primer — 2–4 days per room — PROPOSED ₹99/sq ft [Patna est.: IndiaMART Patna listing ₹150/sq ft; Design Cafe gypsum ₹78/sq ft; metro ref Livspace ₹110–125 + GST; confidence medium]
    • Gypsum cove/designer ceiling — gypsum with cove profile/layers, light cut-outs — 3–5 days per room — PROPOSED ₹125/sq ft [metro ref Livspace premium gypsum ₹120–145 + GST; confidence low-medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: LED panel/cove strip/spot light fixtures, electrical wiring points (per point), final paint coat (often separate), scaffolding for double-height ceilings, GST, removal of old ceiling.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: How is the area measured? A: By the ceiling area in square feet. Cove designs and drops may add running-foot charges, which are listed in the quote.
    • Q: Gypsum or POP: which is better? A: Gypsum is faster and smoother with less mess. POP suits curved or detailed designs but takes longer to set and finish.
    • Q: Can I use false ceiling in the bathroom? A: PVC panels are a better choice for bathrooms and balconies because they resist moisture. Gypsum needs a moisture-resistant board there.
    • Q: Are lights included? A: The price includes cut-outs for lights. Fixtures and wiring points are charged separately so you can pick your own brand.
    • Q: Is painting included? A: Primer is included. The final paint coat is quoted separately unless your package says otherwise.
    • Q: Will it reduce room height? A: Usually by a few inches, depending on design. The technician checks your ceiling height during the visit.
  • Price unit: sq ft
  • Tags/keywords: false ceiling Patna, POP ceiling Patna, gypsum ceiling rate, PVC ceiling, chhat ka design, POP ka kaam, ceiling design with lights, faal ceiling
  • Image brief: Hero — living room with two-level gypsum ceiling and warm cove lighting, evening. Package photo — bathroom/balcony with white PVC panel ceiling and one LED panel.
  • Sources: https://www.livspace.com/in/magazine/false-ceiling-cost — POP, gypsum, PVC metro rates + GST; https://dir.indiamart.com/patna/false-ceiling-services.html — Patna gypsum/POP ₹150/sq ft listing (Jun 2026); https://www.designcafe.com/blog/home-interiors/false-ceiling-cost — gypsum ₹78/sq ft; https://indiframe.com/blog/false-ceiling-cost-india-pop-vs-gypsum — 10x10 POP ₹6,000–12,000; https://www.aajjo.com/product/pop-false-ceiling-2 — POP ₹85/sq ft (Varanasi); https://www.nobroker.in/interiors/design-guides/interior-design-rate-sheet — false ceiling ₹70–150/sq ft.

Renovation & Remodeling (category: Construction & Interior Design)

  • Suggested slug: home-renovation-remodeling | Mode: visit (site inspection + quote) | Status: live (starting estimates)
  • Short description: Bathroom, kitchen or full-home renovation in Patna with an itemised quote after a site visit.
  • Full description: A supervisor visits your home, checks walls, plumbing, waterproofing and wiring, and notes what you want to change. You get an itemised quote covering demolition, civil work, tiling, fittings and debris removal. Bathrooms often need new waterproofing and pipelines in homes older than 10–15 years, so these are checked first. Kitchens can be refreshed with new tiles, granite and a sink, or fully remodelled with modular units. Full-home jobs can bundle painting, flooring, ceilings and electrical upgrades.
  • Bullets:
    • Site inspection of plumbing, leakage, wiring and tiles
    • Itemised quote: demolition, civil, tiling, fittings, debris removal
    • Waterproofing before new tiles in wet areas
    • One supervisor coordinating plumber, mason, electrician and painter
    • Stage-wise payment and clean-up at handover
  • Packages:
    • Bathroom refresh (small, 25–35 sq ft) — tile replacement, basic CP fittings, minor plumbing repair — ~5–7 days [timeline unsourced] — PROPOSED from ₹29,999 [Patna est.: no Patna data; tier-2 estimate ₹22,000–50,000 (≈15–20% below metro); metro ref NoBroker compact ₹25,000–60,000; confidence low]

    • Full home renovation — painting, flooring, bathroom/kitchen work, electrical upgrades — PROPOSED from ₹650/sq ft (carpet area) [no source found for per-sq-ft renovation in Patna; estimate derived from Patna construction finishing share (houseyog) and NoBroker rate sheet items; confidence low—owner must validate with local contractors]
  • Excludes / charged extra: sanitaryware and tiles above the allowance in the quote, modular kitchen cabinets (see Interior Design), structural repairs/beam work, society/RWA permission fees, debris disposal beyond one trip, GST.
  • FAQs:
    • Q: Do I need to vacate the house? A: Not for a single bathroom or kitchen job. For full-home renovation, staying elsewhere for part of the time is often easier.
    • Q: Why do old bathrooms cost more? A: Homes older than 10–15 years often need new waterproofing and plumbing lines, which adds labour and material cost.
    • Q: Can I supply my own tiles and fittings? A: Yes. The quote then covers labour and civil materials only, and you control brand and price.
    • Q: Is there a warranty? A: Workmanship and waterproofing cover terms are written in your quote. [owner to set policy; none sourced]
    • Q: How is the final price fixed? A: After the site visit, you get an itemised quote. Extra work found during demolition is shown and approved by you before it is done.
  • Price unit: job (bathroom/kitchen) / sq ft (full home)

9. Painting

Note: no Patna-specific published rate card was found (searched Patna/Bihar painter rates; Nerolac has a Patna service page but no prices: https://www.nerolac.com/painting-services/bihar/home-painting-services-in-patna). Patna estimates below are derived from tier-2 data: AapkaPainter says tier-2 cities run ~10–15% below metro; UVClaad lists Lucknow/Kanpur (closest tier-2 proxy) labour at ₹6–9 / ₹8–12 per sq ft and ₹22–30 with mid-range material (exterior table). All per-sq-ft figures = PAINTABLE wall+ceiling area, not carpet area (paintable ≈ 3× carpet area).

Full Home Painting (category: Painting)

  • Suggested slug: full-home-painting | Mode: visit (free/low-cost inspection, then doorstep job) | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Repaint or fresh-paint your whole flat or house in Patna, priced by area after a site measurement.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Our painting team visits, measures the walls and ceilings, checks for damp and cracks, and gives a written quote per square foot. You pick the paint grade: economy, premium or luxury emulsion from Asian Paints, Berger, Nerolac or Birla Opus. The price covers furniture covering, light sanding, crack filling, one coat of primer and two top coats. Fresh homes also get two coats of wall putty. Exterior walls are quoted separately because they need weather-grade paint and sometimes scaffolding. Most 2 BHK interiors finish in 4–6 working days.
  • Bullets (3–5, what the customer gets):
    • Site visit with tape measurement and a written, itemised quote (area × rate, brand and product named)
    • Furniture and floor covered, switchboards masked, site cleaned after work
    • 1 coat primer + 2 coats of the chosen emulsion; putty on new or damaged walls
    • Choice of brand and shade; shade card shown during the visit
    • Damp patches flagged and quoted separately as waterproofing
  • Packages (2–4):
    • Economy repaint (Tractor/Bison-class emulsion) — primer + 2 coats on walls & ceilings, minor crack fill — ~3–4 days for 2 BHK (≈2,880 min) — PROPOSED ₹12/sq ft (2 BHK ~2,500 sq ft paintable ≈ ₹29,999 package) [Patna est. ₹10–15/sq ft; metro ref ₹12–18 (AapkaPainter economy), Asian Paints repaint ₹8–12 labour only; confidence medium — tier-2 derived, no Patna card]
    • Premium repaint (Apcolite/Berger Easy Clean-class) — same prep + premium washable emulsion — 4–5 days (≈3,600 min) — PROPOSED ₹18/sq ft (1 BHK ~1,500 sq ft ≈ ₹26,999; 2 BHK ≈ ₹44,999; 3 BHK ~3,800 sq ft ≈ ₹67,999) [Patna est. ₹16–25; metro ref ₹18–28 (AapkaPainter premium); Asian Paints 2 BHK standard emulsion ₹60k–95k metro; confidence medium]
    • Luxury finish (Royale/Silk-class) — premium prep + luxury emulsion with sheen — 5–6 days (≈4,320 min) — PROPOSED ₹27/sq ft [Patna est. ₹25–38; metro ref ₹28–45; confidence medium-low]
    • Exterior repaint (weatherproof emulsion, G+1 house) — wash, crack fill, primer, 2 coats — 4–7 days — PROPOSED ₹20/sq ft, scaffolding extra above ground+1 [Patna est. ₹16–30 (Lucknow proxy ₹22–30 with material); metro ref ₹18–60 (Asian Paints blog); confidence low-medium]
    • Add-on: Fresh painting (new walls, 2 coats putty) — +₹5/sq ft [putty ≈ ₹5/sq ft per AapkaPainter material sheet; confidence medium]
  • Excludes / charged extra: GST (if applicable); waterproofing / seepage treatment; heavy plaster repair; texture, stencil, wallpaper; enamel on doors, grills, windows; scaffolding for exterior above ground+1 (UVClaad: ₹8–15/sq ft metro); shifting heavy furniture out of the house.
  • FAQs (4–6 Q&A, own words, answers ≤ 40 words):
    • Q: How do you calculate the price? A: We measure every wall and ceiling to be painted, minus large doors and windows, and multiply by the per-sq-ft rate of your paint grade. You get the breakup in writing before work starts.
    • Q: Is the inspection visit free? A: The visit is free inside Patna city limits. Outside, a small travel charge applies and is adjusted against the final bill if you book. (Owner to decide — see note.)
    • Q: Can I buy the paint myself? A: Yes, we can do labour-only at a lower rate. You then own any shade mismatch or shortage, so we suggest letting us source it.
    • Q: How long does a 2 BHK take? A: Usually 4–6 working days for interiors, depending on wall condition, putty work and drying time between coats.
    • Q: Do you fix damp walls? A: Painting over damp only peels again. We point out seepage during the visit and quote waterproofing separately before painting.
    • Q: Do I need to empty the rooms? A: No. We move light furniture to the centre and cover it. Please pack fragile items and wall hangings beforehand.
  • Price unit: sq ft (shown as package estimate per BHK)
  • Tags/keywords: home painting Patna, ghar ki putai, ghar painting rate, painter Patna, deewar rangai, putty primer paint, Asian Paints painter, safedi karwana
  • Image brief: Hero — a painter on a short ladder rolling a light pastel emulsion on a living-room wall, furniture wrapped in plastic. Package photo — side-by-side shade card swatches (economy/premium/luxury) on a freshly painted wall.
  • Sources:
    • https://www.asianpaints.com/blogs/painting-cost-per-sq-ft-india.html — interior ₹12–45, exterior ₹18–60/sq ft; labour ₹8–25 (repaint ₹8–12, fresh ₹12–18, premium ₹18–25, texture ₹25+); 1 BHK paintable ~1,400–1,800 sq ft, 2 BHK ~2,500–3,500; 1 BHK cost ₹18k–28k economy / ₹30k–45k standard / ₹50k–75k premium; 2 BHK ₹35k–55k / ₹60k–95k / ₹1–1.8 lakh
    • https://aapkapainter.com/resources/painting-cost-calculator — Oct 2026: labour-only ₹10–14; with material economy ₹12–18, premium ₹18–28, luxury ₹28–45 before 18% GST; tier-2 10–15% lower; exterior 15–25% more; estimate excludes damp treatment/texture; putty ~₹5/sq ft; suggests ≥1-year workmanship warranty (their claim, not an industry standard)

    • https://www.nobroker.in/painting-services/home-painting-ideas/house-painting-cost-in-noida — Noida 2 BHK ₹25k–40k; from ₹12/sq ft basic

  • Inspection visit note: Asian Paints' Beautiful Homes Painting Service advertises a free site visit/inspection (https://www.asianpaints.com/services/asian-paints-safe-painting-service.html); its Bangladesh page mentions a 1-year service warranty only on its top "platinum" plan (https://www.asianpaints.com.bd/services/beautiful-homes-painting-service.html — not verified for India). Recommendation: offer a FREE inspection within Patna municipal limits (₹199 refundable visit fee for Danapur/Phulwari/Hajipur and beyond) — owner to decide. Patna gets heavy monsoon damp; recommend a separate "Waterproofing" item rather than bundling. Room-by-Room Painting (category: Painting)

  • Suggested slug: room-wall-painting | Mode: doorstep | Status: live
  • Short description (≤ 20 words): Paint just one wall, one room or a few rooms, including texture or accent walls, at a fixed starting price.
  • Full description (60–100 words, own words): Not every home needs a full repaint. Book us for a single wall, a bedroom, the kitchen, a bathroom or a balcony. The painter covers your furniture, scrapes loose paint, fills small cracks, applies primer and two coats in the shade you pick. For a feature look, choose an accent wall in a bold colour or a texture finish such as rustic, sand or stencil designs. Small jobs finish in a day; a full room usually takes two days because each coat needs to dry. Final price is confirmed on site if the wall is larger or damaged.
  • Bullets (3–5, what the customer gets):
    • Fixed starting price for a wall, room or small area; final confirmed on site
    • Furniture and floor covering, masking of switches and frames
    • Surface scraping, small crack filling, primer + 2 coats of chosen emulsion
    • Optional texture or stencil accent wall with design catalogue shown at the visit
    • Site cleaned before the painter leaves
  • Packages (2–4):
    • Single wall (up to ~120 sq ft, plain emulsion) — prep + primer + 2 coats — 1 day (≈480 min) — PROPOSED ₹1,799 [Patna est. ₹1,500–2,200 (120 sq ft × ₹12–18 premium + minimum visit); metro ref UC "1 wall painting" from ₹2,499 (9 hrs); confidence medium]
    • One bedroom (walls + ceiling, ~10×12 ft room, ~450 sq ft paintable) — prep + primer + 2 coats premium emulsion — 2 days (≈960 min) — PROPOSED ₹4,499; Kitchen ₹2,799; Bathroom ₹2,299; Balcony ₹2,799 [Patna est. bedroom ₹4,000–6,500; metro ref UC bedroom from ₹5,499, living room ₹6,999, kitchen ₹3,499, bathroom ₹2,999, balcony ₹3,999, store/washing area ₹2,999; confidence medium]
    • Any 2 rooms — 3 days (≈1,440 min) — PROPOSED ₹7,999; Any 3 rooms — ₹10,999 [metro ref UC any 2 rooms from ₹9,999, any 3 rooms from ₹13,999 (3 days); confidence medium]
    • Texture / accent wall (one wall up to ~100 sq ft; rustic, sand or stencil) — base prep + texture + top coat — 1–2 days (≈720 min) — PROPOSED ₹3,999 (₹40/sq ft, ₹4,500+ for metallic/Royale Play-type designer finishes) [Patna est. ₹35–60/sq ft; metro ref texture ₹45–150/sq ft all-in, from ₹50/sq ft (AapkaPainter), ₹40–150 (HomeTriangle), Asian Paints texture labour ₹25+/sq ft; confidence medium-low]
  • Excludes / charged extra: Wall larger than package size (charged per sq ft at the grade rate); damp/seepage treatment; heavy plaster or crack repair; enamel on doors (UC ref from ₹2,199), grills (₹1,999), cabinets (₹1,999), metal gate/shutter (₹2,499); GST if applicable.
  • FAQs (4–6 Q&A, own words, answers ≤ 40 words):
    • Q: What if my room is bigger than the package? A: The painter measures before starting. Extra area is charged at the same per-sq-ft rate, and you approve the new total before work begins.
    • Q: Can you match my existing wall colour? A: Yes, if you share the shade name or code. Otherwise we match by eye using shade cards; an exact match on old, faded paint is not guaranteed.
    • Q: How long before I can use the room? A: Emulsion is touch-dry in a few hours. Keep furniture off the walls for a day and avoid washing the wall for about two weeks.
    • Q: What textures can I choose? A: Common options are rustic, sand, stucco-look, combing and stencil patterns. The painter brings a sample catalogue; complex designer finishes cost more.
    • Q: Is there a smell? A: We use low-odour water-based emulsions by default. Keep windows open; most people sleep in the room the next night.
  • Price unit: job (per wall / per room); texture per sq ft beyond package size
  • Tags/keywords: one room painting Patna, ek deewar paint, kamra rangwana, texture paint wall, accent wall design, bedroom painting, stencil design deewar, painter near me
  • Image brief: Hero — a bedroom with one deep-teal accent wall behind the bed, other walls off-white, drop sheet still on the floor. Package photo — close-up of a hand-trowelled rustic texture wall in warm beige with natural side light.

Brand service vs local Patna painter — comparison note (for Full Home Painting pricing)

  • Berger Express Painting: Berger runs its own painting + waterproofing service and has an online paint cost calculator (carpet area → paintable area → price) — https://www.bergerpaints.com/express-painting, https://www.bergerpaints.com/tools/paint-calculator. No public per-sq-ft rate card on Berger's site.
  • NoBroker (Berger partner page) lists starting rates per sq ft: Tractor UNO ₹7, Tractor Emulsion ₹9, Premium Emulsion ₹15, Royal Luxury Emulsion ₹27 — https://www.nobroker.in/painting-services/berger-paints-painting-services. A NoBroker forum answer says Berger Express starts around ₹28/sq ft and puts a 2 BHK interior at ₹25k–40k — https://www.nobroker.in/forum/how-much-does-berger-express-painting-cost (older, user forum; low weight).
  • Asian Paints Beautiful Homes: free site inspection, supervisor-managed jobs; prices quoted after visit only — https://www.asianpaints.com/services/asian-paints-safe-painting-service.html.
  • Takeaway [INFERENCE]: brand-managed services price at or above metro aggregator rates; local Patna painters usually quote labour-only (₹6–12/sq ft, tier-2 proxy from UVClaad) with the customer buying paint. NoBroker's "starting" figures (₹9 / ₹15 / ₹27) look lower than TiptoBook's proposed all-in ₹12 / ₹18 / ₹27, but it is unclear whether they include putty, primer and two coats, so do not treat them as like-for-like.
